## Gas Pipeline Uniform Accounting Regulations {#gas-pipeline-uniform-accounting-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/83-190
:::

[NATIONAL ENERGY BOARD ACT](/eng/acts/N-7)

Registration 1983-02-25

Regulations Respecting the Uniform System of Accounts of Gas Pipeline
Companies

P.C. 1983-527 1983-02-24
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Energy, Mines and Resources, pursuant to
subsection 88(1) of the [National Energy Board Act](/eng/acts/N-7), is
pleased hereby to approve the revocation of the [Gas Pipeline Uniform
Accounting Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-83-190), C.R.C., c. 1053,
and to approve the annexed [Regulations respecting the uniform system of
accounts of gas pipeline companies](/eng/regulations/SOR-83-190) made by
the National Energy Board.
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875272 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations may be
cited as the [Gas Pipeline Uniform Accounting
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-83-190).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875274 .Part}

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,

[[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2020-50, s.
    6\]]{.Repealed}

[[book cost]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[book cost]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of plant, the
    amount that is recorded in a plant account without any deduction for
    accumulated depreciation or amortization or for any other purpose;
    ([valeur comptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Commission referred to
    in subsection 26(1) of the [Canadian Energy Regulator
    Act](/eng/acts/C-15.1); ([Commission]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[contingent assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[contingent assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means assets without known
    value to the company until fulfilment of conditions regarded as
    uncertain; ([actif éventuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[contingent liabilities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[contingent liabilities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means liabilities
    that may, under certain conditions, become obligations of the
    company but are not direct or assumed obligations on the date of the
    balance sheet; ([passif éventuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to the
    accounts of a company, the calendar year or the fiscal year approved
    by the Commission for the accounts of the company; ([exercice
    financier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[gas plant in service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gas plant in service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means plant used for
    any aspect of pipeline operations or plant held for use under a
    definite plan for future gas pipeline operations; ([installation de
    gazoduc en service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[minor item of plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[minor item of plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an associated part
    or item included within a plant unit; ([article d'installation
    accessoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[net salvage value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[net salvage value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means salvage value minus
    any removal costs; ([valeur nette de récupération]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a company authorized by the
    Commission to operate a gas pipeline; ([exploitant]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[other plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[other plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any installation or
    property that is entirely distinct from and is not operated in
    connection with the gas pipeline service of the company; ([autre
    installation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means in respect of a gas pipeline
    company, any installation or property the cost of which is to be
    recorded in the Plant Accounts listed in Schedule IV;
    ([installation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[plant retired]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[plant retired]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a unit of plant, whether
    it is replaced or not, that is sold, abandoned, demolished,
    dismantled or otherwise removed from gas pipeline service;
    ([installation réformée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[plant unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[plant unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a complete structure,
    apparatus or item of equipment that constitutes a component of any
    installation or property that could properly be included in the
    plant accounts listed in Schedule IV and includes a part of any
    structure or apparatus where such part is a physically distinct part
    of such structure or apparatus and the value of such part is
    material; ([unité d'installation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[salvage value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[salvage value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the amount received for
    plant retired, including insurance proceeds, and includes any amount
    received for material salvage from plant retired where the material
    is sold; ([valeur de récupération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[undivided interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[undivided interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a beneficial
    ownership in common of the assets, liabilities, revenues and
    expenses of a gas pipeline by two or more companies or persons.
    ([copropriété indivise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875296 .Part}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations apply
only to a company authorized by the Commission to construct or operate a
gas pipeline.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240339){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240339.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240671){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240671.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  Group 1 company shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} keep separate books of account in Canada in a
    manner consistent with generally accepted accounting principles;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless otherwise authorized or instructed by the
    Commission, keep accounts in the manner set out in these
    Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} keep a system of accounts as prescribed in these
    Regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every Group 2 company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall keep separate books of account in Canada in a
    manner consistent with generally accepted accounting principles
    until the expiration of one year after such time as the Commission
    grants leave to abandon the operation of the pipeline;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall file a set of audited financial statements
    with the Regulator within one hundred and twenty (120) days after
    the end of each fiscal year of the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall comply with subsections 6(1), (7), (8) and
    (9); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is exempt from complying with these Regulations
    except as prescribed in paragraphs (a) to (c).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section,

  [[Group 1 company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Group 1 company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a company listed in
      Schedule VIII; and ([compagnies du groupe 1]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Group 2 company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Group 2 company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any company, other
      than a Group 1 company, that constructs or operates a gas
      pipeline. ([compagnies du groupe 2]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 2
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240342){.wb-lbx

## [Records]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875313 .Part}

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company's accounting records shall provide sufficient particulars to
  show fully the facts pertaining to all entries made in the accounts.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall maintain the applicable accounts
  listed in Schedules I to VII and may, in addition, keep subsidiary
  accounts for its own purposes.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The account numbers listed in Schedules I to VII
  shall be set out in the descriptive headings of the applicable ledger
  accounts, computer print-outs or other accounting records.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} All transactions applicable to a month shall be
  recorded in the accounts for that month and, at the end of that month,
  trial balances of the accounts shall be prepared.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company shall close its accounts at the end of each
  fiscal year.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Final accounting entries for each month shall be made
  not later than 30 days after the last day of the month, except that
  the final entries for the last month of each fiscal year may be made
  within a period not exceeding 90 days from the end of that last month.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The books, accounts and records referred to herein
  include not only accounting records in a limited technical sense, but
  all records such as minute books, capital stock records, reports,
  correspondence memoranda, computer printouts, tapes and card decks
  that may be useful in determining the history of or facts pertaining
  to any transaction.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} The books, accounts and records shall be readily
  accessible for examination by representatives of the Regulator.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} Where a gas pipeline is owned in undivided interest,
  the operator shall maintain all books, accounts and records of the
  total assets, liabilities, revenues and expenses of the pipeline in a
  manner that permits ready identification and access to the books,
  accounts and records of the beneficial owners of the pipeline.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} The accounts required to be maintained pursuant to
  subsection (2) shall be retained until the expiration of one year
  after leave to abandon the operation of the pipeline has been granted
  by the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/83-884, s. 2

## [Account Grouping]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875327 .Part}

### [Balance Sheet Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875328 .Subheading}

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Balance
  sheet accounts, set out in Schedule I as account series 100 to 293,
  shall, in respect of a company, disclose the company's financial
  position at the balance sheet date.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The balance sheet accounts referred to in
  subsection (1) shall record assets, liabilities, share capital, and
  retained earnings, as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240585){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240585.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Income Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875332 .Subheading}

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Income
  accounts, set out in Schedule II as account series 300 to 341, shall
  show a summary of a company's revenue, expenses and extraordinary
  items.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall inform the Regulator of all
  extraordinary items and shall record them in account 331
  (Extraordinary Income) or account 341 (Extraordinary Income
  Deductions), as applicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company's net balance of income or loss shall be
  transferred to retained earnings at the end of each fiscal year.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[extraordinary
  item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means material gain or loss that is not
  typical of a company's normal business activities, is not expected to
  occur regularly over a period of years and would not be considered a
  recurring factor in any evaluation of the ordinary operating process
  of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 4
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240434){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240434.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240586){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240586.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Retained Earnings Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875340 .Subheading}

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** Retained earnings accounts,
set out in Schedule III as account series 350 to 357, shall show a
summary of all changes in balance sheet account 212 (Retained Earnings)
for each year and shall include

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the balance transferred from income for the year;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prior period adjustments;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} miscellaneous retained earnings adjustments;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} dividend appropriations; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} income tax applicable to retained earnings
  adjustments.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 5(F)
:::

### [Plant Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875349 .Subheading}

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Plant
  accounts, set out in Schedule IV as account series 401 to 498, are
  subsidiary to balance sheet accounts 100 (Gas Plant in Service), 101
  (Gas Plant Leased to Others), 102 (Gas Plant Held for Future Use) and
  107 (Improvement to Facilities Leased from Others), and shall be used
  to classify a company's plant.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Plant accounts shall be supported by subsidiary
  accounts that record the costs, by location, of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each major facility including each main gathering
    system, products extraction plant, gas storage facility, compressor
    station and main pipeline; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other facility that is separately located.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Plant accounts shall be maintained to record
  separately expenditures for each plant addition, replacement and
  retirement authorized by the Commission.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any installation or property not used in pipeline
  operations shall be recorded in balance sheet account 110 (Other
  Plant).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240347){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240347.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240587){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240587.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Operating Revenue Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875357 .Subheading}

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** Operating revenue
accounts, set out in Schedule V as account series 500 to 579, shall be
used to classify all revenue of a company from pipeline operations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240589){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240589.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Operating and Maintenance Expense Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875359 .Subheading}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Operating expense accounts, set out
  in Schedule VI as account series 610 to 729, shall include records of
  a company's operating expenses pertaining to pipeline operations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Maintenance expense accounts, set out in Schedule VII
  as accounts series 810 to 889, shall include records of a company's
  maintenance expenses pertaining to pipeline operations.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operating and maintenance expenses referred to in
  subsections (1) and (2) shall be recorded in the appropriate account
  under the applicable service function referred to in Schedule VI and
  VII.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The service functions referred to in subsection (3)
  are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} gathering;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} products extraction;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} gas supply (operating only);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} underground storage;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} transmission;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} general; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} administrative and general (operating only).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Expenses common to more than one operating or
  maintenance expense account shall, where practicable, be equitably
  apportioned between such accounts and adequate records showing the
  basis of such apportionment shall be maintained.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The operating and maintenance expense accounts under
  the service functions listed in paragraphs (4)(a) to (f) shall be
  supported by subsidiary accounts that record the expenses, by location
  as practicable for each major facility such as each main gathering
  system, products extraction plant, gas storage facility, compressor
  station, main pipeline system and any other facility that is
  separately located.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 6(E)
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240590){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240590.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Clearing Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875376 .Subheading}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  it is not possible to record expenditures directly to a particular
  expense or plant account, a company may maintain clearing accounts for
  recording the expenditures.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Amounts recorded in clearing accounts shall be
  regularly distributed to the appropriate expense or plant accounts on
  an equitable basis.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any balances in the clearing accounts shall be
  substantially distributed before the end of each fiscal year unless
  items held therein relate to a future period.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The net monthly balance in clearing accounts shall be
  included in account 179 (Other Deferred Debits) or account 279 (Other
  Deferred Credits), as applicable.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where an operator maintains clearing accounts for the
  purpose of distributing monthly balances to the beneficial owners of a
  pipeline, the accounts shall contain sufficient particulars to permit
  ready identification and reconciliation with the accounts of the
  beneficial owners.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240592){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240592.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Interpretation of Lists of Items]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875383 .Subheading}

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  item that appears in a representative list in any account in the
  schedules shall be included in the corresponding account of a company
  if the inclusion of the item is consistent with the text of the
  account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A representative list shall be considered only as
  representative and not as excluding from an account any analogous
  component or item that is omitted from the list.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240594){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240594.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Plant Acquired or Constructed]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875387 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875388 .Subheading}

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall record in the plant accounts the costs incurred to
  acquire or construct any plant.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the consideration given for plant referred to
  in subsection (1) is other than money, the money value of the
  consideration on the day the contract for the acquisition or
  construction comes into force shall be debited to the applicable
  accounts and a record of the consideration, in sufficient detail to
  identify it, shall be retained by the company.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a company purchases all or part of the existing
  facilities of another company, the purchasing company shall prepare
  and submit to the Commission for approval details of the proposed
  method of accounting for such purchase together with the company's
  reasons for the proposed method of accounting.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the facilities referred to in subsection (3)
  are purchased from an affiliated company, the original cost of the
  facilities to the affiliated company and the accumulated depreciation
  applicable to the facilities to the date of purchase, as shown in the
  accounts of the affiliated company, shall be recorded in the accounts
  of the purchasing company.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Balance sheet account 115 (Gas Plant Under
  Construction) shall be supported by subsidiary records that record
  separately each transaction for each project involving plant
  additions, replacements or relocations.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where a plant acquired by a company is constructed by
  or for that company, the costs to be recorded in the plant accounts
  shall include the elements of cost set out in sections 16 to 27 and
  section 29.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240350){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240350.html"

### [Cost of Labour]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875396 .Subheading}

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cost
  of labour for construction of plant shall include the amount paid for
  labour, including employee benefits, performed by the company's
  employees.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the employees referred to in subsection (1) are
  specially assigned to construction work, their pay while so engaged
  shall be included in the cost of the work.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No charge shall be made to plant accounts for the pay
  of the company's employees whose services in connection with
  construction are merely incidental, except as provided in section 26.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The allocation of travelling and other incidental
  expenses of the company's employees shall be made to accounts on the
  same basis as the distribution of the pay of those employees.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240597){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240597.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cost of Materials and Supplies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875402 .Subheading}

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cost
  of materials and supplies shall include the purchase price of
  materials and supplies at the point of free delivery, the cost of [ad
  valorem]{.otherLang lang="la"} taxes, inspection, transportation and,
  where appropriate, a suitable proportion of store expenses.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In determining the cost of materials and supplies,
  suitable allowance shall be made for all discounts allowed and
  realized on the purchase thereof.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In debiting the plant accounts with materials and
  supplies used, allowance shall be made for the value of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unused portions and other salvage;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the materials recovered from temporary pipe,
    scaffolding and other temporary structures used in construction; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} small tools recovered and usable for other
    purposes.

### [Cost of Special Machine and Heavy Work Equipment Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875410 .Subheading}

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cost
  of special machine and heavy work equipment service shall include the
  cost of labour, material, depreciation, supplies and other costs
  incurred in maintaining and operating power shovels, scrapers, pile
  drivers, dredgers, ditchers, material loaders and similar equipment,
  and any amount paid to others for rent, operation and maintenance of
  such equipment.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a construction project necessitates the
  purchase of equipment to be used exclusively on the project until the
  project is completed, the cost of the equipment shall be debited to
  the accounts representing the cost of the project and while the cost
  remains so debited no debit shall be made to expenses for depreciation
  on the equipment.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount realized from any subsequent sale of
  equipment referred to in subsection (2), or the appraised value of any
  equipment retained after the completion of the project for which it
  was purchased, shall be credited to the accounts debited with the
  equipment's cost.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The appraised value of equipment retained after the
  completion of the project for which it was purchased shall be debited
  to the appropriate plant account and, thereafter, for the purposes of
  accounting, that appraised value shall be considered as the cost of
  the equipment.

### [Cost of Transportation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875416 .Subheading}

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cost
  of transportation incurred in the construction of plant shall include
  the cost of transporting men, equipment and material and supplies used
  for construction purposes.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cost of transportation of construction material
  to the point where material is received by the company shall be
  included, if practicable, in the cost of the material.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240599){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240599.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cost of Contract Work]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875420 .Subheading}

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** Cost of contract work
shall include any amount paid for work performed under contract by other
companies and individuals.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240670){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240670.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cost of Insurance, Injuries and Damages]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875422 .Subheading}

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cost
  of insurance, injuries and damages shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the premiums paid for insuring plant during
    construction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs incurred for protection against fire and
    wilful destruction of plant during construction; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the costs incurred in respect of injuries to
    persons, damage to property of others and damage to plant incidental
    to construction.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Insurance recovered or recoverable for compensation
  for injuries to persons injured during the construction of plant shall
  be credited to the account or accounts debited with the cost of the
  injuries.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Insurance recovered or recoverable in respect of
  property damage incidental to the construction of plant shall be
  credited to the account or accounts debited with the cost of the
  damage.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), any injury or damage
  incidental to the removal of a structure or part thereof shall be
  debited to the account recording the retirement costs of the
  structure.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any injury or damage incidental to the removal of a
  structure or part thereof that is an encumbrance on newly acquired
  land by a company shall be included in the cost of that land.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 7(E)
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240600){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240600.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cost of Privileges]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875434 .Subheading}

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** Cost of privileges shall
include compensation paid for the temporary use of public and private
property in connection with a construction project.

### [Cost of Engineering Services]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875436 .Subheading}

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** Cost of engineering
services shall include any amount paid to other companies, firms or
individuals engaged by the company to plan, design, prepare estimates,
supervise, inspect or give general advice and assistance in connection
with a construction project.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240602){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240602.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Earnings and Expenses During Construction]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875438 .Subheading}

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** Earnings and expenses
during the construction of plant shall include all revenues derived
during the construction period from property that is included in the
cost of plant under construction and all expenses that are attributable
to the revenues received.

### [Cost of Rents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875440 .Subheading}

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Cost of rents shall
include any amount paid for the use of construction quarters and office
space occupied by construction forces.

### [Cost of Overhead During Construction]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875442 .Subheading}

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cost
  of overhead during construction shall include engineering, supervision
  and administrative salaries and expenses, employee benefits, legal
  fees, taxes, shop service and other analogous items, to the extent
  that they may be reasonably considered to be plant costs.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Only actual and reasonable overhead costs shall be
  assigned to particular jobs or units.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The records supporting the entries for cost of
  overhead during construction shall show the total amount of each type
  of overhead expenditure capitalized in the year and the basis of
  distribution thereof in accordance with the instructions set out in
  account 498 (Overhead Charged to Construction).

### [Allowance for Funds Used During Construction]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875447 .Subheading}

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may, at its option, capitalize an allowance for funds used
  during construction.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The allowance for funds used during construction
  shall be calculated in accordance with the instructions set out in
  account 497 (Allowance for Funds Used During Construction), and may,
  at the option of the company, be prorated over the appropriate
  depreciable plant accounts.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240604){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240604.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Date Placed in Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875451 .Subheading}

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the
  date that plant is placed in service, the company shall cease to
  capitalize an allowance for funds used during construction on the
  plant and shall compute and charge to the appropriate account an
  amount representing depreciation as determined under sections 49 to
  57.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The date that the plant is in service, for the
  purpose of subsection (1), shall not be later than the first day of
  the month following issuance of a Leave to Open Order by the
  Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 8
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240352){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240352.html"
  role="button"}

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240605.html"
  role="button"}

[Previous
Version](section-28-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Analogous Elements of Cost]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875457 .Subheading}

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Elements of cost analogous to those
  defined in sections 16 to 27 may be included in the cost of plant.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The costs incurred in disposing of material excavated
  may be included as an element referred to in subsection (1).

## [Additions to Plant]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875461 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875462 .Subheading}

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of these Regulations, additions to plant shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} increases in plant through purchase or construction
    of additional plant,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the replacement of existing plant units, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the capitalization of major renewals to plant

  and, subject to the approval of the Commission, may include
  substantial improvements made to existing plant in order to make such
  plant more useful, more efficient, more durable or of greater capacity
  than the plant was when it was originally placed in service.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cost of additions referred to in subsection (1)
  shall be debited to the appropriate plant accounts.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Typical elements of construction are set out in
  sections 16 to 27 and section 29.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} All expenditures relevant to new construction shall
  be capitalized.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} After the completion of initial construction, only
  the costs directly connected with additions or replacements of plant
  shall be capitalized.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} All property charged to plant accounts shall consist
  of plant units or minor items of plant.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240355){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240355.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Plant Units]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875474 .Subheading}

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  company shall establish its own list of plant units for each plant
  account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company's list of plant units shall be filed with
  and be subject to the approval of the Commission.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the approval of the Commission, the list
  of plant units, as filed, may be revised and amended from time to
  time.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The cost of an additional plant unit shall be debited
  to the appropriate plant account.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The book cost of a plant unit retired, whether
  replaced or not, shall be credited to the appropriate plant account.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where a plant unit is replaced, the book cost of the
  original plant unit shall be credited to the appropriate plant account
  and the cost of the replacement unit shall be debited to the
  appropriate plant account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240359){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240359.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240606){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240606.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Minor Items of Plant]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875482 .Subheading}

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 33, the cost of a
  minor item of additional plant not constituting a replacement but
  identified with a plant unit shall be debited to the appropriate plant
  account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cost of a minor item of plant that constitutes a
  replacement shall be debited to the appropriate expense account and
  the book cost of the minor item replaced shall not be adjusted.

### [Minimum Rules for Minor Items of Plant]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875486 .Subheading}

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a minor item of plant is acquired at a cost of less than \$5,000, the
  cost shall be debited to expense.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} combine unrelated items of plant for the purpose of
    excluding the items from the provisions of subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} divide expenditures made under a general plan for
    related items that cost \$5,000 or more per item into smaller
    parcels for the purpose of debiting capital items to expense.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} With the approval of the Commission after the filing
  of a request by a company, the company may, for the purpose of its
  accounting, adopt a limit of less than the amount set out in
  subsection (1) for any class of plant.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No change may be made in the amount of a limit
  referred to in subsection (3) except with the prior approval of the
  Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240363){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240363.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240607){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240607.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 33"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Major Renewals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875494 .Subheading}

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[cost of renewals]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
  aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of material, other than old parts
    remaining in a rebuilt plant unit, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of labour used in the rebuilding process,

  but does not include the cost of dismantling and repairing old parts
  that are re-used.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), where the cost of renewals
  to parts of a plant unit exceeds 50 per cent of the new replacement
  cost of a plant unit of the same kind and class, the plant unit shall
  be considered as rebuilt.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to the renewal of a
  plant unit where the new replacement cost of that unit would not
  exceed \$120,000.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A rebuilt plant unit shall be accounted for as an
  addition and the old plant unit that the rebuilt plant unit replaced
  shall be accounted for as retired from service.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), the charge to the
  appropriate plant account for a rebuilt plant unit shall be the
  aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the value of old parts remaining in the rebuilt
    plant unit; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of labour and material applied.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The cost of dismantling and repairing old parts used
  in the renewal of plant shall be debited to maintenance expense.

### [Second-Hand Plant]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875507 .Subheading}

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** Where second-hand plant
not previously owned by the company is acquired in a physical condition
that necessitates extensive expenditures to bring it up to the standard
required by the company, the expenditures shall be debited to the
appropriate plant account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240609){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240609.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-35-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 35"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Retirements of Plant]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875509 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875510 .Subheading}

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a plant unit, whether replaced or not, is retired from pipeline
  operations, the book cost of the plant unit shall be credited to the
  appropriate plant account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The book cost and the costs of removal of a
  depreciable plant unit retired and not replaced shall be debited to
  account 105 (Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant) or account 106
  (Accumulated Amortization --- Gas Plant), as applicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The net salvage value of a plant unit retired shall
  be credited to the accumulated depreciation or accumulated
  amortization account referred to in subsection (2).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Each retirement project shall be supported by
  subsidiary records that show separately the details thereof.

### [Book Cost]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875516 .Subheading}

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** Where the book cost of
any retired plant is not recorded separately, the book cost of that
plant shall be its appropriate share of the book cost of the entire
group in which the particular plant is located.

### [Salvage Value]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875518 .Subheading}

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  salvaged material is retained for use by a company, the original cost,
  estimated if not known, of the material, less a fair allowance for
  depreciation, shall be debited to account 150 (Plant Materials and
  Operating Supplies).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The salvage value of depreciable plant or salvaged
  material therefrom shall be credited to account 105 (Accumulated
  Depreciation --- Gas Plant) or account 106 (Accumulated Amortization
  --- Gas Plant), as applicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The removal costs incurred in dismantling or
  demolishing retired depreciable plant and in recovering salvage
  therefrom shall be debited to account 105 (Accumulated Depreciation
  --- Gas Plant) or account 106 (Accumulated Amortization --- Gas
  Plant), as applicable, except that the current cost of removing and
  replacing a minor item of plant in maintenance operations shall be
  included in the appropriate expense account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240611){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240611.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Ordinary Retirement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875523 .Subheading}

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  respect of depreciable plant, [[ordinary
  retirement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a retirement of depreciable
  plant that results from causes reasonably assumed to have been
  anticipated or contemplated in prior depreciation or amortization
  provisions.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} There shall be no debit or credit to income or to
  retained earnings for an ordinary retirement.

### [Extraordinary Retirement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875527 .Subheading}

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  respect of depreciable plant, [[extraordinary
  retirement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a retirement of depreciable
  plant that results from causes that could not reasonably have been
  anticipated or contemplated in previously making provision for
  depreciation or amortization, including such causes as fire, storm,
  flood, premature obsolescence or unexpected and permanent shutdown of
  an entire operating assembly for reasons other than ordinary wear and
  tear.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the gain or loss on an extraordinary retirement
  is material, the company shall inform the Regulator and shall transfer
  the amount of the gain or loss from account 105 (Accumulated
  Depreciation-Gas Plant) or account 106 (Accumulated Amortization-Gas
  Plant) to account 331 (Extraordinary Income) or account 341
  (Extraordinary Income Deductions), as applicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), a company may, with
  the approval of the Commission, transfer all or part of the amount of
  a material gain or loss on an extraordinary retirement to account 279
  (Other Deferred Credits) or account 171 (Extraordinary Plant Losses),
  as applicable, for amortization at a rate approved by the Commission.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Immaterial gains or losses resulting from
  extraordinary retirements shall be accounted for in the same way as
  ordinary retirements.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 9
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240365){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240365.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240436){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240436.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240612){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240612.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-40-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 40"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Pipeline Relocations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875535 .Subheading}

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a plant unit of pipeline is relocated, it shall be considered as plant
  retired and its book cost shall be credited to the appropriate plant
  account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a plant unit of pipeline has been relocated,
  the relocated plant unit shall be considered as an addition and the
  cost shall be debited to the appropriate plant account.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a company's pipeline or any part thereof is
  located in accordance with an agreement that may require the company
  to relocate all or part of its pipeline and the circumstances are such
  that the company has no reasonable alternative but to relocate more
  than a plant unit of pipeline, the company may, with the approval of
  the Commission, debit the costs of relocation to expenses for the
  period in which the relocation was carried out.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a relocation of a type referred to in
  subsection (3) results from action by a governmental authority, the
  cost of relocation may be accounted for in the manner set out in
  subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240368){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240368.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240613){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240613.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Line Pipe Replacements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875541 .Subheading}

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a plant unit of line pipe is replaced with other pipe in the same
  location, a company shall debit to the appropriate plant account the
  cost of opening and back filling the trench together with the cost of
  hauling, laying and connecting the pipe, the cost of removing retired
  pipe from the trench and other costs of pipeline construction.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cost of reconditioning line pipe not removed
  shall be accounted for as repairs and not as retirements or
  replacements.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the plant unit of line pipe referred to in
  subsection (1) is not replaced by new pipe in the same location, the
  cost of opening and back filling the trench from which the pipe is
  removed together with the cost of removing the pipe shall be accounted
  for as cost of salvage.

::: HistoricalNote

  role="button"}

:::

### [Other Plant]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875546 .Subheading}

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  plant is no longer required for pipeline purposes but is retained by
  the company, its book cost shall be transferred to account 110 (Other
  Plant) and the accumulated depreciation or accumulated amortization,
  if any, with respect thereto shall be transferred from account 105
  (Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant) or account 106 (Accumulated
  Amortization --- Gas Plant) to account 111 (Accumulated Depreciation
  --- Other Plant) or account 112 (Accumulated Amortization --- Other
  Plant), as applicable.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where depreciable other plant included in account 110
  (Other Plant) is retired or sold, the book cost, salvage and removal
  costs shall be recorded in account 111 (Accumulated Depreciation ---
  Other Plant).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the gain or loss from the sale or retirement of
  depreciable other plant is material, the company shall inform the
  Regulator and shall transfer the amount of the gain or loss from
  account 111 (Accumulated Depreciation --- Other Plant) or account 112
  (Accumulated Amortization --- Other Plant) to account 331
  (Extraordinary Income) or account 341 (Extraordinary Income
  Deductions), as applicable.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the gain or loss from the sale or retirement of
  depreciable other plant included in account 110 (Other Plant) is not
  material, the company shall transfer the amount of the gain or loss
  from account 111 (Accumulated Depreciation --- Other Plant) or
  account 112 (Accumulated Amortization --- Other Plant) to account 319
  (Other Income) or account 329 (Other Income Deductions), as
  applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 10
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240438){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240438.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240616){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240616.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-43-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 43"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Land Retired or Sold]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875554 .Subheading}

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  land is retired or sold, account 100 (Gas Plant in Service),
  account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others), account 102 (Gas Plant Held
  for Future Use) or account 110 (Other Plant) shall be credited with
  the book cost of the land.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the gain or loss from the sale or retirement of
  land is material, the company shall inform the Regulator and shall
  transfer the amount of the gain or loss to account 331 (Extraordinary
  Income) or account 341 (Extraordinary Income Deductions), as
  applicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the gain or loss from the sale or retirement of
  land is not material, the company shall transfer the amount of the
  gain or loss to account 319 (Other Income) or to account 329 (Other
  Income Deductions), as applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 11
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240440){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}

  role="button"}

## [Maintenance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875561 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875562 .Subheading}

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** The cost of repairs
recorded in the maintenance accounts for gas plant shall include

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of inspection to determine the repairs that
  are necessary;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of adjusting, repairing or replacing parts;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cost of inspection, testing and running of parts
  to determine whether or not the repairs have been properly made and
  the repaired item is ready for service.

<!-- -->

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Maintenance accounts kept in
  accordance with Schedule VII shall be debited with the costs of
  repairing and maintaining plant and shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} incidental costs including the construction and
    removal of false work in connection with maintenance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of relocating pipeline plant where
    retirement accounting for units of plant is not involved;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cost of raising or lowering line pipe in the
    trench where retirement accounting for units of plant is not
    involved;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the cost of repairing fences, sidewalks, driveways
    and streets within or adjacent to the plant;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the cost of installing, maintaining and removing
    temporary facilities to prevent any interruptions in operations; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the cost of maintaining public improvements
    included in account 176 (Public Improvements).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where employees whose pay is normally debited to
  operating expenses

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} mow and beautify grounds and buildings,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} periodically restore seasonal features such as
    gardens, shrubbery and lawns, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} clear and remove snow, ice and fallen timber,

  their pay and any other costs arising from such employment may be
  debited to the maintenance or operating account, at the option of the
  company.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240619){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240619.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Equalization of Maintenance Expenses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875580 .Subheading}

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** Where a company prepares
a budget for its maintenance expenses for a fiscal year, the following
procedure may be followed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the actual monthly expenses shall be debited to the
  appropriate maintenance accounts;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the excess of an actual expense over a budgeted
  monthly expense shall be debited to account 275 (Gas Cost and
  Maintenance Equalization) and credited to the appropriate expense
  account;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the excess of a budgeted expense over an actual
  monthly expense shall be debited to the appropriate expense account
  and credited to account 275 (Gas Cost and Maintenance Equalization);
  and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} at the end of the fiscal year, the balance in
  account 275 (Gas Cost and Maintenance Equalization) shall be
  transferred to the appropriate expense account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240621){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240621.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Depreciation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875586 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875587 .Subheading}

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** In sections 49 to 57,

[[group system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[group system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a system by which a
    weighted average rate of depreciation is calculated for a particular
    group of plant accounts, a plant account, or a group of assets
    within a plant account, and established in recognition of the fact
    that some part of the investment in a group of assets may be
    recovered through salvage realization and that there will be
    variations in the service lives of the assets constituting the
    group, even among assets of the same class; ([système de
    dépréciation en groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[service life]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[service life]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period of time
    between the placement of plant in service and its retirement; ([vie
    utile]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[service value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[service value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the book cost of plant
    minus the estimated net salvage value of that plant. ([valeur de
    service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Under
  the group system, in the case of an ordinary retirement of an
  individual asset in a group of assets, the accumulated depreciation
  attributable to the asset shall, for the purposes of these
  Regulations, be considered to be equal to the cost of the asset minus
  any amount that may reasonably be recoverable through salvage
  realization, whether or not the actual service life of the asset is
  shorter or longer than the anticipated average service life for the
  group.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Assets, within a group of assets, remaining in use
  after reaching their average service life expectancy shall not be
  regarded as fully depreciated until actual retirement or until the
  group is fully depreciated, whichever is earlier.

<!-- -->

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Charges for depreciation with
  respect to accounts that are classed by section 51 as being accounts
  covering assets that are depreciable shall be computed in conformity
  with the group system.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall charge depreciation by using such one
  of the following methods as is chosen by the company and approved by
  the Commission for use by the company:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the straight line method;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use or unit of production method;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the diminishing value method; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other systematic method consistent with
    generally accepted accounting principles.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 12
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240370){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240370.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240622){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240622.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-50-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Depreciable Assets]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875604 .Subheading}

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  plant accounts, with the exception of accounts 410, 420, 450, 460 and
  480 (Land), are classed as accounts covering assets that are
  depreciable and, for the purpose of the group system of depreciation
  accounting, depreciable asset accounts may be grouped according to the
  nature of the plant included in each account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Accounts 401 (Franchises and Consents) and 402 (Other
  Intangible Plant) are depreciable only to the extent of items included
  therein that are subject to recognizable loss in value.

### [Depreciation Charges]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875608 .Subheading}

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  shall be debited each month to expenses or other appropriate accounts
  and credited to the accounts for accumulated depreciation amounts that
  will allocate, in a systematic and rational manner, the service value
  of plant over its estimated service life.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Monthly depreciation charges under the straight line
  method shall be computed by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applying the annual percentage rate of depreciation
    to the depreciation base as of the first of each month and dividing
    the result by 12; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with the prior approval of the Commission, applying
    the annual percentage rate of depreciation to the depreciation base
    at the beginning of the company fiscal year and dividing the result
    by 12.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Monthly depreciation charges under the use or unit of
  production method shall be computed by applying the appropriate rate
  or use or production per unit for the year to the number of units of
  use or production for the month.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Monthly depreciation charges under the diminishing
  value method shall be computed by applying the annual percentage rate
  to the depreciation base as of the first of each month and dividing
  the result by 12.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company shall compute depreciation in accordance
  with subsection 28(1).

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The monthly debits for depreciation of plant included
  in account 100 (Gas Plant in Service), account 101 (Gas Plant Leased
  to Others) or account 102 (Gas Plant Held for Future Use) shall be
  debited to account 303 (Depreciation).

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Depreciation on assets included in account 110 (Other
  Plant) shall be debited to account 311 (Expense of Other Plant).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 13
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240372){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240372.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240623){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240623.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Rates of Depreciation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875621 .Subheading}

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  separate depreciation rate shall be used in computing depreciation
  charges for each group of plant accounts, each plant account or each
  group of assets within a plant account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In determining the rate of depreciation,
  consideration shall be given to all relevant factors including
  variations in use, increasing obsolescence or inadequacy.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each depreciation rate used shall be approved by the
  Commission; but, where no rate has previously been approved for any
  class of plant, an interim rate as estimated by the company shall be
  used.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240375){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240375.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240624){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240624.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-53-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 53"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} As
  soon as the information can be assembled, a company shall file with
  the Regulator depreciation rates estimated to be appropriate for each
  group of plant accounts, each plant account or each group of assets
  within a plant account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The rates referred to in subsection (1) shall be
  based on the service value and estimated service life of plant, as
  developed by a study of the company's history and experience and such
  engineering and other information as may be available with respect to
  future operating conditions.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The rates referred to in subsection (1) shall be
  established to produce a charge for depreciation equal to the sum of
  the amounts that would otherwise be chargeable as depreciation for
  each of the various classes of plant included in a group of plant
  accounts, for each plant account or for each group of assets within a
  plant account.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The rates referred to in subsection (1) may be
  developed by a company by the method deemed most appropriate for the
  portrayal of the depreciation experienced and, when filed, shall be
  accompanied by a statement showing their basis and the methods
  employed in their computation.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company shall, at any time, upon direction of the
  Commission, conduct a study on the suitability of depreciation rates
  in use in the light of the company's history and available engineering
  data, and shall submit to the Commission for approval a report of the
  results of such study together with recommendations for any desired
  changes in depreciation rates.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240378){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240378.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240442){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240442.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240625){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240625.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-54-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 54"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where,
  in the opinion of a company, depreciation rates that have been filed
  with the Regulator are no longer applicable, the company shall file
  revised rates with the Commission for approval.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a company acquires plant for which no
  depreciation rates have been approved by the Commission, the company
  shall immediately compile and submit to the Regulator its estimate of
  the appropriate depreciation rates, developed in accordance with the
  provisions of sections 53 and 54.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 7](#1239605-1240327){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239605-1240327.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-55-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 55"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Accumulated Depreciation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875635 .Subheading}

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated depreciation shall be
  subdivided to show separately the amount applicable to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each group of plant accounts,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each plant account, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each group of assets within a plant account,

  for which a separate weighted average rate of depreciation has been
  established.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the amount is material, accumulated
  depreciation applicable to the assets in one depreciation group shall
  not be transferred by a company to another depreciation group without
  the approval of the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240381){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240381.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-56-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Plant Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875643 .Subheading}

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** A company shall keep
records of depreciable plant and plant retirements in sufficient detail
to show the service life of plant that has been retired and to permit
the service life of plant to be estimated by the use of the mortality
method or other appropriate method.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240627){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240627.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-57-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 57"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Amortization]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875645 .Subheading}

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 59 and 60, [[amortization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
gradual recovery of an amount included in account 100 (Gas Plant in
Service), account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others), account 102 (Gas
Plant Held for Future Use) or account 110 (Other Plant) by distributing
such amount over a fixed period or over the estimated remaining life of
the plant.

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** Where it is anticipated
by a company that plant will be abandoned owing to the exhaustion of a
particular source of traffic, obsolescence or any other cause, the
company shall not change from depreciation accounting to amortization
accounting without first obtaining the authorization of the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240384){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240384.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240628){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240628.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 59"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  monthly debits for amortization of gas plant included in account 100
  (Gas Plant in Service), account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others) or
  account 102 (Gas Plant Held for Future Use) shall be debited to
  account 304 (Amortization).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Amortization on assets included in account 110 (Other
  Plant) shall be debited to account 311 (Expense of Other Plant).

### [Insurance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875651 .Subheading}

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Insurance premiums paid to insurance
  companies shall be debited to account 723 (Insurance), unless the
  premiums are chargeable to clearing accounts or relate to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the construction of pipeline facilities; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} employee benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Insurance costs relative to the construction of
  pipeline facilities shall be debited [pro rata]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  to the appropriate plant accounts.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Insurance costs relative to employee benefits shall
  be debited to account 725 (Employee Benefits).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any amount recovered from the insurance referred to
  in subsection (1), (2) or (3) shall be credited to the account or
  accounts originally debited with the related loss or expense.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a company elects to create and maintain
  reserves for self-insurance, account 723 (Insurance) shall be debited
  with estimated amounts in lieu of commercial insurance premiums, and
  account 290 (Insurance Appropriations) shall be credited with the
  estimated amounts.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} A schedule of risks covered by self-insurance shall
  be kept showing the character of the risk covered and the rates used
  to compute estimated amounts referred to in subsection (5).

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The rates referred to in subsection (6) shall not
  exceed commercial rates for the same protection.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Where, as a result of an event or accident covered by
  self-insurance, costs are incurred of a type that would normally be
  debited to an expense account, such costs shall be debited to the
  insurance appropriations account and any excess of the expenditure
  over the applicable self-insurance shall be debited to the appropriate
  expense account.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} Where the self-insurance schedule referred to in
  subsection (6) covers the retirement of plant, the accounting for the
  retirement shall be as outlined in section 36 and the self-insurance
  applicable to the retired item shall be transferred from account 290
  (Insurance Appropriations) to account 105 (Accumulated Depreciation
  --- Gas Plant) or account 106 (Accumulated Amortization --- Gas
  Plant), as applicable.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} Where a company insures with a commercial insurance
  company any risks covered by self-insurance, the premiums for the
  insurance policy shall be debited to the insurance appropriations
  account and any recoveries under the policies shall be credited
  thereto.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 14(E)
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240629){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240629.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-61-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Funds and Appropriations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875667 .Subheading}

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cash,
  securities or other assets set aside for a specific purpose shall be
  debited to account 122 (Sinking Funds), account 123 (Miscellaneous
  Special Funds) or account 131 (Special Deposits), as applicable, and
  the appropriate asset account shall be credited.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Income from assets held in account 122 (Sinking
  Funds) and account 123 (Miscellaneous Special Funds) shall be credited
  to account 316 (Income from Sinking and Other Funds).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a mortgage or any contractual obligation
  entered into by a company requires that income from assets held in a
  fund be added to that fund, the company shall make the necessary
  transfer to the fund account.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a transfer referred to in subsection (3) is to
  account 123 (Miscellaneous Special Funds), and represents a company's
  contribution to account 291 (Welfare and Pension Appropriations) or to
  account 290 (Insurance Appropriations), the company shall concurrently
  debit account 329 (Other Income Deductions) and credit account 291 or
  290, as applicable, with the amount transferred.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company's contribution to account 291 (Welfare and
  Pension Appropriations) or to account 290 (Insurance Appropriations)
  shall be provided by debits to expenses.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where the gain or loss on the sale of assets recorded
  in account 122 (Sinking Funds) or account 123 (Miscellaneous Special
  Funds) is material, the company shall inform the Regulator and shall
  transfer the gain or loss to account 331 (Extraordinary Income) or
  account 341 (Extraordinary Income Deductions), as applicable.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where the gain or loss on the sale of assets referred
  to in subsection (6) is not material, the company shall transfer the
  gain or loss to account 316 (Income from Sinking and Other Funds) or
  account 329 (Other Income Deductions), as applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 15
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240444){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240444.html"
  role="button"}

## [Securities Owned]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875678 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875679 .Subheading}

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section and sections 64 to 68, [[cost]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
  the amount of money paid by a company to acquire securities or, where
  the consideration paid for the securities is other than money, the
  money value of the consideration at the time the securities are
  acquired.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall record the cost of its investment in
  securities, excluding amounts paid for accrued interest and accrued
  dividends, in the appropriate accounts at the time of acquisition of
  the securities.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/86-998, s. 16\]]{.Repealed}

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where securities having a fixed maturity date and
  recorded in account 132 (Temporary Cash Investments), account 120
  (Investments in Affiliated Companies), account 121 (Other
  Investments), account 122 (Sinking Funds) or account 123
  (Miscellaneous Special Funds) are purchased at a discount or premium,
  that discount or premium may be amortized over the remaining life of
  the securities by periodic debits or credits to the account in which
  the cost of the securities is recorded, with corresponding credits or
  debits to account 314 (Income from Investments), account 315 (Income
  from Affiliated Companies) or account 316 (Income from Sinking and
  Other Funds), as applicable, and if the amount to be amortized does
  not exceed \$1,000, a company may write off the total discount or
  premium at one time.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No amortization entries shall be recorded in respect
  of discounts on securities held as investments unless there is reason
  to believe that the securities will be disposed of at a sum equal to
  their par value, or that the par value will be collected at maturity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 16
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240631){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240631.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-63-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 63"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Temporary Cash Investments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875688 .Subheading}

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the gain or loss on the sale of assets recorded in account 132
  (Temporary Cash Investments) is material, the company shall inform the
  Regulator and shall transfer the amount of the gain or loss to account
  331 (Extraordinary Income) or account 341 (Extraordinary Income
  Deductions), as applicable.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A gain or loss on a sale referred to in
  subsection (1) that is not material shall be transferred to
  account 314 (Income from Investments).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 17
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240446){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240446.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240632){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240632.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-64-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 64"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the amount required to provide for reductions in the market value of
  temporary cash investments is material, the company shall inform the
  Regulator and shall debit the amount required to account 341
  (Extraordinary Income Deductions) and concurrently credit account 132
  (Temporary Cash Investments).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the amount required to provide for reductions
  in the market value of temporary cash investments is not material, it
  shall be debited to account 314 (Income from Investments) and
  concurrently credited to account 132 (Temporary Cash Investments).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 18
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240448){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}

  role="button"}

### [Investments in Affiliated Companies and Other Investments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875699 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)

\]
:::

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the gain or loss on the sale of assets recorded in account 120
  (Investments in Affiliated Companies) or account 121 (Other
  Investments) is material, the company shall inform the Regulator and
  shall transfer the amount of the gain or loss to account 331
  (Extraordinary Income) or account 341 (Extraordinary Income
  Deductions), as applicable.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A gain or loss on a sale referred to in
  subsection (1) that is not material shall be transferred to
  account 319 (Other Income) or to account 329 (Other Income
  Deductions), as applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 19
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240450){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240450.html"

  role="button"}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 66"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall be governed by recognized accounting principles in
  reducing the value at which securities are recorded in account 120
  (Investment in Affiliated Companies) or account 121 (Other
  Investments) to reflect anticipated loss in value.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Permanent impairment of the value of securities
  referred to in subsection (1) shall be recorded in the accounts but
  fluctuations in market value shall not be recorded.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a reduction in the value of securities referred
  to in subsection (1) is material, the company shall inform the
  Regulator and shall debit the amount of the reduction to account 341
  (Extraordinary Income Deductions).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a reduction in the value of securities referred
  to in subsection (1) is not material, the company shall debit the
  amount of the reduction to account 326 (Provision for Loss in
  Valuation of Investments).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 20

  role="button"}

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240635.html"
  role="button"}
