::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the approval of the
  Commission, a company may write down its investment in a separately
  incorporated company controlled by the company to reflect the
  company's share of the losses of the separately incorporated company,
  where the operation of such a company is considered to be an integral
  part of the company's gas transportation system.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall credit the amount of a write-down
  referred to in subsection (1) to account 126 (Allowance for Loss in
  Value of Investments) and debit that amount to account 326 (Provision
  for Loss in Valuation of Investments) unless the amount of the
  write-down is material, in which case it shall be debited to
  account 341 (Extraordinary Income Deductions).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the approval of the Commission, where a
  company provides for a loss in accordance with this section and the
  separately incorporated company makes a profit in a subsequent year,
  the controlling company shall adjust the allowance for losses recorded
  in account 126 (Allowance for Loss in Value of Investments) by
  debiting the amount of the profit to that account and concurrently
  crediting account 326 (Provision for Loss in Valuation of Investments)
  unless the profit is material, in which case it shall be credited to
  account 331 (Extraordinary Income).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240388){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}

  role="button"}
:::

## [Securities Issued]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875716 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875717 .Subheading}

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  sections 70 to 74,

  [[discount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[discount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the excess of the par or
      stated value of any security issued or resold over the value of
      the consideration received for the security;
      ([escompte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[expense]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[expense]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any commission paid for marketing equity and
        debt securities,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of preparing and distributing
        prospectuses,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cost of preparing certificates and other
        similar documents, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} legal fees in respect of the issuance of
        securities; ([dépenses]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[premium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[premium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the excess value of the
      consideration received from the issue or resale of securities over
      the par or stated value of the securities.
      ([prime]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a prospectus includes the issuance of long-term
  debt and capital stock, the items of expense that are distinguishable
  as to debt or stock shall be segregated and the remaining expenses
  shall be apportioned by using the ratio that the proceeds of long-term
  debt or capital stock bear to the total proceeds.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Separate ledger accounts shall be maintained for each
  class or subclass of securities.

### [Share Capital]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875729 .Subheading}

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Premiums received on the issuance of
  par value shares shall be credited to account 210 (Contributed
  Surplus).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cost of issuing shares shall be debited to
  account 177 (Share Capital Expense) and subsequent costs of issuing
  shares shall be debited to account 728 (Other General Expense).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/86-998, s. 21\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 21
:::

### [Long-term Debt]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875736 .Subheading}

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  total discount and expense or the total premium less expense, as the
  case may be, associated with each series of each class of long-term
  debt shall be recorded in a separate subaccount of account 170
  (Unamortized Debt Discount and Expense) or account 270 (Unamortized
  Debt Premium and Expense).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} All the debit balances in the subaccounts referred to
  in subsection (1) shall be considered as part of the balance in
  account 170 (Unamortized Debt Discount and Expense).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} All the credit balances in the subaccounts referred
  to in subsection (1) shall be considered as part of the balance in
  account 270 (Unamortized Debt Premium and Expense).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the total discount and expense or the total
  premium less expense applicable to any particular issue of securities
  does not exceed \$25,000, a company, at the time of issue, may debit
  the entire amount to account 321 (Amortization of Debt Discount,
  Premium and Expense), as applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240638){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240638.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-71-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 71"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** In each fiscal period of
a company, there shall be debited to account 321 (Amortization of Debt
Discount, Premium and Expense), and credited to account 170 (Unamortized
Debt Discount and Expense), a portion of each of the debit balances
included in account 170 and the calculation of that portion shall be
based on the ratio of the fiscal period to the remaining life of the
respective securities, calculated from the beginning of the fiscal
period to the date of maturity of the debt to which the charges relate,
and correspondingly there shall be credited to account 321 (Amortization
of Debt Discount, Premium and Expense) and debited to account 270
(Unamortized Debt Premium and Expense), a similar portion of each of the
credit balances included in account 270.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240640){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240640.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-72-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 72"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  any issue or series of long-term debt of a company is redeemed before
  its maturity date, otherwise than by exchange or conversion into
  shares, the amount of the unamortized debt discount and expense or
  unamortized premium less expense applicable to the portion of the debt
  redeemed shall be credited to account 170 (Unamortized Debt Discount
  and Expense) or debited to account 270 (Unamortized Debt Premium and
  Expense), as applicable, and where the amount is not material,
  concurrently debited or credited to account 321 (Amortization of Debt
  Discount, Premium and Expense) as applicable, in the year of
  redemption.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the amount referred to in subsection (1) is
  material, the company shall inform the Regulator and shall debit the
  amount to account 341 (Extraordinary Income Deductions) or credit the
  amount to account 331 (Extraordinary Income), as applicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections (1) and (2), where an
  issue or series of long-term debt of a company is redeemed before its
  maturity date by refunding through the issuance of new long-term debt,
  the company may, where the amount is not material, amortize the amount
  of unamortized discount and expense or unamortized premium less
  expense applicable to the portion of the debt redeemed, by regular
  debits or credits as applicable, to account 321 over a period not
  exceeding the lesser of the remainder of the original life of the
  issue or series redeemed or the life of the new long-term debt.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the amount referred to in subsection (3) is
  material, the company shall inform the Regulator and shall debit the
  amount to account 341 (Extraordinary Income Deductions), or credit the
  amount to account 331 (Extraordinary Income), as applicable.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where an issue or series of long-term debt of a
  company is redeemed before its maturity date by exchange for or
  conversion into capital stock of the company, the manner of accounting
  for the transaction shall be subject to the prior approval of the
  Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 22
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 11](#1239613-1240390){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239613-1240390.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240455){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240455.html"
  role="button"}

### [Company Long-term Debt Owned]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875751 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)

\]
:::

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  any long-term debt is reacquired and included in account 221 (Company
  Long-term Debt Owned), and the amount of the difference between the
  amount paid upon reacquisition and the par value plus the applicable
  unamortized premium or minus the applicable unamortized discount and
  expense, as the case may be, is not material, the amount of the
  difference shall be debited to account 329 (Other Income Deductions)
  or credited to account 319 (Other Income), as applicable.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the amount of the difference referred to in
  subsection (1) is material, the company shall inform the Regulator and
  shall debit the amount of the difference to account 341 (Extraordinary
  Income Deductions) or credit that amount to account 331 (Extraordinary
  Income), as applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 23
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240457){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240457.html"
  role="button"}

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240642.html"
  role="button"}

## [Current Assets and Liabilities]{.HTitleText1} {#h-875757 .Part}

### [Current Assets]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875758 .Subheading}

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), current
  assets shall include cash and other assets that are not restricted
  from use for current operations and, in the normal course of
  operations, are expected to be converted into cash or consumed in the
  production of income within a one-year period.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Materials and supplies shall be included in current
  assets notwithstanding that they may not be consumed in a one-year
  period.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Current assets that are of doubtful value shall be
  written down or written off to the appropriate income or operating
  expense accounts to an extent required to adjust their value, but
  uncollectable accounts receivable shall be debited to account 145
  (Allowance for Doubtful Accounts) to the extent that an allowance has
  been provided therefor.

### [Current Liabilities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875763 .Subheading}

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Current liabilities shall include
  obligations that are payable on demand or that mature or become due
  within one year.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), loans payable that
  have a maturity date within a one-year period and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have been incurred primarily for the construction
    of plant, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} will be replaced by long-term financing,

  may be included in account 249 (Other Long-term Debt).

### [Accrued Assets and Liabilities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875770 .Subheading}

- **[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where,
  during any month, a transaction has occurred that affects the accounts
  of a company but the amount involved cannot be determined with
  accuracy at the end of that month, the amount shall be estimated and
  included in the proper accounts, unless it would not appreciably
  affect the accounts.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the actual amount involved in a transaction
  referred to in subsection (1) has been finally determined, the
  estimated amount referred to in that subsection shall be adjusted to
  show the actual amount.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/86-998, s. 24\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 24
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240646){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240646.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-77-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 77"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prior Period Adjustments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875777 .Subheading}

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where,
  in any fiscal year of a company, the amount of an adjustment to the
  income of a company for a prior fiscal year is material and the amount
  of the adjustment

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is specifically identified with and directly
    related to the business activities of a particular prior fiscal
    year,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not attributable to economic events or
    obsolescence occurring subsequent to the date of the financial
    statements for such prior fiscal year,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} depends primarily on decisions or determinations by
    persons other than the company, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} could not be reasonably estimated prior to such
    decisions or determinations,

  the company shall inform the Regulator and shall record the amount of
  the adjustment in account 351 (Prior Period Adjustments).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, in any fiscal year of a company, the amount of
  an adjustment to the income of a company for a prior fiscal year is
  not material, the company shall include the amount of the adjustment
  in the same accounts in which it would have been recorded if it had
  been recorded in that prior fiscal year.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the following shall
  be applied in determining materiality:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} items of a similar nature shall be considered in
    the aggregate and dissimilar items shall be considered individually;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to qualify for inclusion as a prior period item,
    the item should exceed the greater of one per cent of the total
    operating revenue and 10 per cent of the balance transferred from
    utility income accounts to account 350 (Balance Transferred from
    Income) for the year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 25
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 12](#1239656-1240459){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239656-1240459.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240647){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240647.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Contingent Assets and Liabilities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875791 .Subheading}

- **[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/86-998, s.
  26\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The par value of securities or the total value of
  other obligations for which a company with other parties is jointly
  and severally liable shall be stated as a liability only in the amount
  that was not primarily assumed by the other parties under the terms of
  the agreement by which the company and the other parties become
  jointly and severally liable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount by which the value referred to in
  subsection (2) exceeds the liability stated or subsequently
  established according to the agreement shall be shown as a contingent
  liability.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-998, s. 26
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240648){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240648.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-79-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 79"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Affiliated Companies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-875798 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)

\]
:::

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of these Regulations, one company is affiliated with
  another company if one of them is the subsidiary of the other or both
  are subsidiaries of the same company or each of them is controlled by
  the same person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a company is a
  subsidiary of another company if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is controlled by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that other company,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that other company and one or more companies
      each of which is controlled by that other company, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two or more companies each of which is
      controlled by that other company; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is a subsidiary of a company that is subsidiary
    to that other company.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a company is
  controlled by another company or person or by two or more other
  companies if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares of the company carrying more than 50 per
    cent of the votes for the election of directors are held, otherwise
    than by way of security only, by or for the benefit of that other
    company or person or by or for the benefit of such other companies;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the votes carried by shares referred to in
    paragraph (a) are sufficient, if exercised, to elect a majority of
    the board of directors of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240650){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240650.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}

#### [Text Pertaining to Balance Sheet Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#text-pertaining-to-balance-sheet-accounts .SchedHeadL2}

##### [Assets]{.HTitleText3} {#assets-1 .SchedHeadL3}

[Plant]{.HTitleText4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [100]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Plant in Service*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include a company's investment
    in plant, property and equipment used in gas pipeline service other
    than that included in account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others),
    account 102 (Gas Plant Held for Future Use) and account 107
    (Improvements to Facilities Leased from Others).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The balance in this account shall be classified
    according to Schedule IV and shall be supported by subsidiary
    records in which the plant is subdivided into component parts
    corresponding to

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each group of plant accounts;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each plant account; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each asset or group of assets within a plant
      account.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The records referred to in subsection (2) shall
    show in detail the current debits and credits.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the consideration given for plant is other
    than cash, the value of such consideration shall be determined on a
    cash basis and the entry recording such transaction shall describe
    the actual consideration with sufficient particularity to identify
    it.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} The company shall furnish the Regulator at its
    request with the particulars of the determination of the cash value
    of any consideration referred to in subsection (4).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [101]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Plant Leased to Others*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of gas plant
    owned but leased to others as operating units or systems, where the
    lessee has exclusive possession.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The balance in this account shall be classified
    according to Schedule IV.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [102]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Plant Held for Future Use*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the investment in plant,
    property and equipment in existence at the date of the balance sheet
    and held for future use under a definite plan for gas pipeline
    operations.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The balance in this account shall be classified
    according to Schedule IV.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [103]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Retirement Work in Progress*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} When plant included in accounts 100, 101, 102 and
    107 is retired, this account shall be debited with

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the book cost of the plant retired, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs of removal, demolition and dismantling
      of plant,

    and the account shall be credited with amounts recovered from
    salvage, including insurance proceeds.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} When final costs of each retirement are
    established, the book cost and the costs of removal less amounts
    recovered shall be transferred to account 105 (Accumulated
    Depreciation --- Gas Plant) or account 106 (Accumulated Amortization
    --- Gas Plant), as applicable.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [105]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be credited with amounts of
    depreciation concurrently debited to account 303 (Depreciation) to
    cover the loss in service value of depreciable gas plant, the cost
    of which is included in account 100 (Gas Plant in Service),
    account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others) or account 102 (Gas Plant
    Held for Future Use).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where applicable, this account shall be credited
    with the depreciation of amounts included in account 107
    (Improvements to Facilities Leased from Others).

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} When the final costs of retirement of depreciable
    gas plant are established, this account shall be debited with

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the book cost of the plant retired, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs of removal, demolition or dismantling
      of the plant retired,

    and the account shall be credited with amounts recovered from
    salvage, including insurance proceeds.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be debited or credited with
    amounts transferred to another account as a result of an
    extraordinary retirement. (*See* section 40 of these Regulations)

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} For general ledger and balance sheet purposes, this
    account shall be regarded and treated as a composite reserve, but
    for the purposes of analysis, a company shall maintain subsidiary
    records in which accumulated depreciation is subdivided into
    component parts corresponding to

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each group of plant accounts,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each plant account, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each group of assets within a plant account,

    and the records shall show in detail the current debits and credits.
    (*See* sections 48 to 57 of these Regulations)

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be subdivided as follows:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant in
      Service;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant Leased to
      Others;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant Held for
      Future Use; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Depreciation --- Improvements to
      Facilities Leased from Others.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [106]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accumulated Amortization --- Gas Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} When a company is authorized by the Commission to
    amortize amounts carried in account 100 (Gas Plant in Service),
    account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others) or account 102 (Gas Plant
    Held for Future Use), this account shall be credited with amounts
    concurrently debited to account 304 (Amortization). (*See* sections
    59 and 60 of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where applicable, this account shall also be
    credited with the amortization of amounts included in account 107
    (Improvements to Facilities Leased from Others).

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a change from depreciation accounting to
    amortization accounting is authorized by the Commission with respect
    to any group of plant accounts, plant account, or group of assets
    within a plant account, the applicable balance in account 105
    (Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant), shall be transferred to
    this account.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} When the final costs of retirement of amortizable
    gas plant are established, this account shall be debited with

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the book cost of the plant retired, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs of removal, demolition or dismantling
      of the plant retired,

    and the account shall be credited with amounts recovered from
    salvage, including insurance proceeds.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be debited or credited with
    amounts transferred to another account as a result of an
    extraordinary retirement. (*See* section 40 of these Regulations)

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsidiary records shall be maintained for each
    group of plant items under a separate amortization authorization.

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be subdivided as follows:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Amortization --- Gas Plant in
      Service;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Amortization --- Gas Plant Leased to
      Others;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Amortization --- Gas Plant Held for
      Future Use; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Accumulated Amortization --- Improvements to
      Facilities Leased from Others.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [107]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Improvements to Facilities Leased from Others*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the unamortized or
    undepreciated cost of improvements made to facilities leased from
    others, where the improvements are made for the purpose of gas
    pipeline operations.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any amounts refunded to the company by the lessor
    in connection with the improvements referred to in subsection (1)
    shall be credited to this account.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The balance in this account shall be classified
    according to Schedule IV.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the service life of the improvements is
    terminable by the action of the lease, the cost of the improvements,
    less net salvage, shall be spread over the life of the lease by
    debits to account 304 (Amortization) and concurrent credits to
    account 106 (Accumulated Amortization --- Gas Plant).

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the service life of the improvements is not
    terminated by the action of the lease, the cost of the improvements,
    less net salvage, shall be accounted for as depreciable plant by
    debits to account 303 (Depreciation) and concurrent credits to
    account 105 (Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [110]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include investment in plant that
    is entirely distinct from and is not operated in connection with the
    gas pipeline service of a company.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where other plant is retired from service, this
    account shall be credited with the cost of the plant retired.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Other Plant to be
    included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock2">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Commercial power plants
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock2">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Land and buildings not used in pipeline service
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock2">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Land and property acquired and held in anticipation of an indefinite
    future use
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock2">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Mineral and timber lands
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [111]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accumulated Depreciation --- Other Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be credited with accounts of
    depreciation concurrently debited to account 311 (Expenses of Other
    Plant) to cover loss in service value of depreciable plant, the cost
    of which is included in account 110 (Other Plant).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the time of retirement of depreciable other
    plant, this account shall be debited with

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of the plant retired, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs of removal, demolition or dismantling
      of the plant retired,

    and the account shall be credited with amounts recovered from
    salvage, including insurance proceeds.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [112]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accumulated Amortization --- Other Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be credited with amounts of
    amortization concurrently debited to account 311 (Expenses of Other
    Plant), when a company is required to amortize amounts carried in
    account 110 (Other Plant).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where there is a change from depreciation
    accounting to amortization accounting, the applicable balance in
    account 111 (Accumulated Depreciation --- Other Plant) shall be
    transferred to this account.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} At the time of retirement of amortizable other
    plant, this account shall be debited with

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the book cost of the plant retired, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs of removal, demolition or dismantling
      of the plant retired,

    and the account shall be credited with amounts recovered from
    salvage, including insurance proceeds.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [115]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Plant Under Construction*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of construction
    of gas plant not yet placed in service, including

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of land acquired for such projects;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unapplied construction materials and supplies;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} taxes during construction; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} other elements of construction cost.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} When the construction of any plant included in this
    account has been completed and the plant has been placed in service,
    the applicable cost shall be credited to this account and debited to
    account 100, 101, 102 or 107, as appropriate.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [116]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Plant Under Construction*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of construction of plant other
  than plant under construction that may be included in account 115 (Gas
  Plant Under Construction).

###### [Investments]{.HTitleText4} {#investments .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [120]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Investments in Affiliated Companies*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of a company's
    investment in securities issued or assumed by affiliated companies
    and investment advances made to affiliated companies. (*See*
    sections 63 and 66 to 68 of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be maintained in a manner that
    will enable information on the investment in each affiliated company
    to be submitted under the following headings:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bonds and debentures;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} other secured obligations;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} unsecured notes;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} investment advances;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} interest accrued on any of the above when the
      interest is not subject to current settlement; and

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} miscellaneous investments.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where any securities referred to in subsection (2)
    are pledged, a complete record thereof shall be maintained.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [121]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Investments*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of a company's investment in securities
      issued or assumed by non-affiliated companies, other than
      securities held in special deposits or special funds; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} investment advances made to non-affiliated
      companies and individuals. (*See* sections 63, 66 and 67 of these
      Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be maintained in a manner that
    will enable information on the investment in each non-affiliated
    company to be submitted under the following headings:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bonds and debentures;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} other secured obligations;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} unsecured notes;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} investment advances;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} interest accrued on any of the above when the
      interest is not subject to current settlement; and

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} miscellaneous investments.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where any securities referred to in subsection (2)
    are pledged, a complete record thereof shall be maintained.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [122]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Sinking Funds*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include cash and the cost of
    securities and other assets, held by trustees or by the company's
    treasurer, that have been segregated in distinct funds and are for
    the purpose of providing resources for the redemption of debt or
    shares.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A separate account shall be kept for the sinking
    funds applicable to each class of debt or share issue.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} An appropriate record shall be maintained for
    securities issued or assumed by the company and held in sinking
    funds. (*See* section 62 of these Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [123]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Miscellaneous Special Funds*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include cash and the cost of
    securities and other assets, held by trustees, managers or by the
    company's treasurer, that have been segregated in funds for
    insurance, employees' pensions, savings, relief, hospital and other
    purposes not provided for elsewhere.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A separate account shall be kept for each fund.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} This account shall not include funds that the
    company holds solely as trustee and in which it has no beneficial
    interest. (*See* section 62 of these Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [126]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Allowance for Loss in Value of Investments*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include all credit balances
    maintained by a company where those credit balances represent
    reductions in the book value of investments carried in account 120
    (Investments in Affiliated Companies) and account 121 (Other
    Investments). (*See* account 326 (Provision for Loss in Valuation of
    Investments))

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Debits to this account that represent reductions in
    the allowance for loss in value of investments carried in account
    120 or 121 shall be made only with the prior approval of the
    Commission.

###### [Current]{.HTitleText4} {#current .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [130]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Cash*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} commercial bank accounts available for general
    chequing purposes, as distinguished from bank accounts provided for
    special purposes, such as special deposits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} petty cash funds; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} funds received by a company, recorded as cash
    receipts and undeposited at the close of the accounting period.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [131]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Special Deposits*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the unexpended balances of funds deposited
    specifically for the payment of dividends, interest and other
    current liabilities; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} other deposits subject to current withdrawal for
    specific purposes only, including amounts deposited with airlines,
    utilities and unemployment insurance commissions, etc.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [132]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Temporary Cash Investments*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of securities acquired for the
  purpose of temporarily investing cash, such as government treasury
  bills, deposit receipts, short-term notes and other investments of a
  short-term nature. (*See* sections 63 to 65 of these Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [140]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accounts Receivable --- Trade*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include amounts receivable for the transportation,
  storage and delivery of gas, exclusive of amounts receivable from
  affiliated companies.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [141]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accounts Receivable --- Other*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the following items when the amounts
  involved are considered collectible:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} advances to officers, employees and agents as
    working funds;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} amounts due from corporations, firms and
    individuals;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the book value of notes receivable or other similar
    evidence of money receivable, due within one year from the date of
    issue; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} other similar items.

  (*See* section 75 of these Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [142]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accounts Receivable --- Affiliated Companies*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include all amounts due from
    affiliated companies, such as trade accounts, dividends and
    interest, where such amounts are subject to settlement within one
    year from the date of the balance sheet. (*See* subsection 80(1) of
    these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be maintained in a manner that
    will enable information on receivables from each affiliated company
    to be submitted under the following headings:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} secured obligations;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unsecured notes;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} advances;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} debit balances in open accounts;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} interest accrued on any of the above where such
      interest is subject to current settlement; and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} miscellaneous receivables.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [145]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Allowance for Doubtful Accounts*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the total of the
    balances maintained by a company for the purpose of providing for
    reductions in the value of current assets recorded in

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} account 140 (Accounts Receivable --- Trade),

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} account 141 (Accounts Receivable --- Other),

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} account 142 (Accounts Receivable --- Affiliated
      Companies), and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} account 162 (Other Current Assets),

    and corresponding debits shall be made to account 728 (Other General
    Expense).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Collections on accounts that were previously
    debited to this account shall be credited to this account.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [147]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Interest and Dividends Receivable*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of interest accrued to the date of the
      balance sheet on bonds owned,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of interest accrued to the date of the
      balance sheet on loans made, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amounts of dividends declared on shares
      owned,

    except where the interest or dividend receivable is from an
    affiliated company.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} No dividends or other returns on securities issued
    or assumed by the accounting company shall be included in this
    account.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [150]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Plant Materials and Operating Supplies*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of materials
    purchased primarily for use in construction, operations or
    maintenance, such as shop material, articles in process of
    fabrication by the accounting company, spare parts, fittings,
    valves, appliances, line pipe and other supplies but shall not
    include materials properly includable in account 115 (Gas Plant
    Under Construction) or account 102 (Gas Plant Held for Future Use).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Materials and supplies purchased shall be debited
    to this account at the actual costs of the materials and supplies at
    point of free delivery plus transportation costs, [ad
    valorem]{.otherLang lang="la"} taxes and any other relevant costs,
    and in determining the cost of materials and supplies, suitable
    allowance shall be made for all discounts allowed in the purchase
    thereof.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Materials and supplies issued shall be credited to
    this account and concurrently debited to the appropriate
    construction, operating or maintenance expense account on the basis
    of a unit price, determined by the use of cumulative average, first
    in-first out, or such other method of inventory accounting as
    conforms with accepted accounting practices consistently applied.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Material recovered in connection with construction,
    maintenance work or the replacement or demolition of plant and
    classified as reusable shall be debited to this account at its
    original cost, estimated if not known, and credited to the
    appropriate maintenance, construction or accumulated depreciation
    account.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Material recovered in connection with construction,
    maintenance work or the replacement or demolition of plant and
    classified as scrap shall be debited to this account on the basis of
    its fair and reasonable value and credited to the appropriate
    maintenance, construction or accumulated depreciation account.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} When salvaged material is sold at a higher or lower
    price than that at which it is included in this account, an
    appropriate adjustment shall be made in the accounts that were
    credited when the material was recovered and debited to this
    account.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [151]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Material and Supplies*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the value of all unapplied material for
  current non-gas pipeline operations and maintenance, such as spare
  parts, shop material and supplies held primarily for other plant,
  merchandising, jobbing and contract work.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [152]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Stored Underground --- Available for Sale*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of gas purchased or produced and stored
      in depleted or partially depleted gas or oil fields or other
      underground reservoirs and held for use in meeting gas service
      requirements of customers; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the value of that portion of the stored gas in
      excess of the amount that may properly be included in account 458
      (Base Pressure Gas).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Gas included in this account shall be valued at
    average cost on a consistent basis, but gathering and transmission
    expenses incurred through the use of company facilities in moving
    gas to the storage area and expenses of storage facilities shall not
    be included in the cost except as may be authorized by the
    Commission.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Deliveries to or withdrawals from underground
    storage of gas held for use to satisfy gas requirements of customers
    shall be debited or credited to this account, as appropriate.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [153]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Transmission Line Pack Gas*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of gas owned by the company in its
  own pipelines or in non-owned pipelines and used to maintain the line
  pressure required for the transmission of gas, but the gathering and
  transmission expenses incurred through the use of company facilities
  in moving gas to the transmission area shall not be included except as
  authorized by the Commission.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [160]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Prepayments*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include amounts representing
    prepaid gas, expenses and other similar items that are to be debited
    to appropriate accounts by systematic write-off.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be maintained in a manner that
    will disclose the amount of each class of prepayment and write-off.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [162]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Current Assets*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include current assets not provided for elsewhere.

###### [Deferred Debits]{.HTitleText4} {#deferred-debits .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [170]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Unamortized Debt Discount and Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the total of the debit
    balance in the subaccounts maintained for each series of each class
    of long-term debt in accordance with section 69.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amounts recorded in this account shall be
    amortized in accordance with section 72 or 73, as applicable.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [171]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Extraordinary Plant Losses*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include material losses
    authorized by the Commission to be transferred from accumulated
    depreciation or accumulated amortization accounts to this account,
    in accordance with subsection 40(3).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before an amount is transferred to this account, a
    company shall provide the Commission with full details of the
    calculation thereof together with the future accounting treatment
    proposed by the company.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Amounts recorded in this account shall be amortized
    by systematic debits to account 304 (Amortization), or otherwise
    disposed of as the Commission may approve or direct.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [172]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Preliminary Survey and Investigation Charges*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be debited with all expenditures
    for preliminary surveys, plans, investigations and similar items
    made for the purpose of determining the feasibility of projects for
    gas pipeline service, and with the costs associated with
    applications for certificates of public convenience and necessity,
    Commission hearings, the acquisition of options to purchase land,
    land rights, easements and similar items for use in contemplated
    projects.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, as a result of the expenditures referred to
    in subsection (1), a plant is acquired or constructed, this account
    shall be credited and the appropriate accounts debited with the
    costs related to that plant.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a project referred to in subsection (1) is
    not proceeded with, the costs included in this account shall be
    transferred to account 329 (Other Income Deductions), unless the
    amount is material, in which case the company shall inform the
    Regulator and, unless otherwise directed by the Commission, shall
    debit the amount to account 341 (Extraordinary Income Deductions).

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Records supporting entries to this account shall be
    maintained in a manner that will enable complete information to be
    available as to the nature and purpose of the expenditures.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [173]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Work in Progress*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include all costs that are incurred for other work
  in progress and that may not be included in account 115 (Gas Plant
  Under Construction) or account 116 (Other Plant Under Construction).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [176]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Public Improvements*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the deferred portion of assessments by
      governmental authorities (by mutual agreement or otherwise) to
      cover the cost of constructing public improvements, when a company
      has elected to make payment by lump sum and not by instalments
      over a number of years; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost borne by a company of public
      improvements constructed by it under governmental requirements.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Amounts that are no longer deferred shall be
    debited to account 305 (Municipal and Other Taxes) and credited to
    this account.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Public Improvements
    to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <ul>

    relocation of street or highway
    </div>
    </div></li>

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">

    </div>

    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [177]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Share Capital Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall, with respect to the original
    and subsequent issues of share capital or with respect to any shares
    split, include expenses of the following type:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of preparing and distributing
      prospectuses;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of soliciting subscriptions for shares;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fees and commissions paid to agents, lawyers and
      accountants;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the cost of preparing and issuing certificates of
      shares;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} expenses incurred in securing legislative and
      regulatory approval for increases or decreases in authorized
      capital; and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} other similar items.

    (*See* sections 68 to 70 of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The expenses of the type indicated in
    paragraph (1)(d) with respect to the subsequent issues of share
    certificates shall be debited to account 728 (Other General
    Expense).

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Amounts recorded in this account shall be
    amortized, or otherwise disposed of, as the Commission may approve
    or direct.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [178]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Organization Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include expenditures incidental
    to organizing a company, such as

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all fees paid to governments for the privilege of
      incorporation;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} legal fees; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} other similar items.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Amounts recorded in this account shall be
    amortized, or otherwise disposed of, as the Commission may approve
    or direct.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [179]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Deferred Debits*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} expenditures that cannot be disposed of until
    further information is received;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} undistributed balances in clearing accounts;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} variances in estimated and actual foreign exchange
    expenditures arising from foreign currency service of debt
    securities issued by the company and variances between estimated and
    actual rates of foreign exchange realized from repurchases of debt
    securities issued by the company where such a deferral of foreign
    exchange variances has been directed by the Commission to facilitate
    consideration of disposition at a later date; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} expenditures of a deferred nature, not provided for
    elsewhere, that are to be amortized over future periods.

##### [Liabilities]{.HTitleText3} {#liabilities-1 .SchedHeadL3}

###### [Shareholders Equity]{.HTitleText4} {#shareholders-equity .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [200]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Share Capital*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total par value of any class of shares, if
      applicable, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total amount received from the issuance of
      each class of shares without par value, excluding amounts that
      have been properly allocated to account 210 (Contributed Surplus),

    where the shares have been issued to [bona fide]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} purchasers and has not been reacquired and cancelled.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall also include appropriations of
    retained earnings that have been transferred to share capital.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} When share capital is reduced or cancelled, this
    account shall be debited with the appropriate amount applicable to
    the particular series or class of shares involved.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be subdivided in a manner that
    will show the total amount credited to this account with respect to
    each series or class of shares issued and outstanding and carrying
    different conditions.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} An appropriate record shall be maintained showing
    the total number of shares issued and outstanding for each series or
    class referred to in subsection (4).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [210]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Contributed Surplus*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} surplus arising from transactions relating to the
      company's share capital; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} capital contributions and donations from
      government and other sources.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Contributed Surplus
    to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations.)

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Premium received on the issuance of par value
      shares prior to December 15, 1975 ([Canada Business Corporations
      Act](/eng/acts/C-44), S.C. 1974-75-76, c. 33) or a prior date
      where a Company elected to be subject to the Act

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Proceeds of sale of donated shares

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Credits resulting from the redemption or
      conversion of shares at less than the amount recorded as share
      capital

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Other contributions made by shareholders in
      excess of par or stated value of shares

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} The portion of proceeds from the issuance of no
      par value shares that has been properly allocated to this account

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} Capital contributions in the form of subsidies,
      building sites, etc.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [212]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Retained Earnings*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the balance, debit or credit, of the
  amounts included in the Retained Earnings accounts listed in
  Schedule III to these Regulations.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [216]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Excess of Appraised Value of Plant Over Depreciated Cost*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be credited with the increase in
    value of fixed assets as a result of an appraisal, where the
    appraisal is associated with

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an actual purchase and sale of fixed assets, as
      in the acquisition of an affiliated company;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reorganization of the accounting company; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a substantial issue of securities, whether equity
      shares or otherwise.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The use of this account and the disposition of any
    amounts included herein shall be subject to the prior approval of
    the Commission.

###### [Long-term Liabilities]{.HTitleText4} {#long-term-liabilities .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [220]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Long-term Debt*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total par value of unmatured debt maturing
      more than one year from the date of the balance sheet, where such
      debt is issued and not retired or cancelled; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total par value of similar unmatured debt of
      other companies, the payment of which debt has been assumed by the
      company.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amounts in this account shall be divided to
    show the par value of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} certificates or other evidences of long-term
      debt, pledged and unpledged, held in the company's treasury by its
      agents or trustees or otherwise subject to its control; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} certificates or other evidences of long-term debt
      issued and outstanding, and not held by the company, its agents or
      trustees, or subject to its control.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amounts included in this account shall be
    further divided to show the amount of each of the following classes
    of long-term debt:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} mortgage bonds --- bonds secured by lien on
      physical property and not included in any other subdivisions of
      this account;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} collateral trust bonds --- bonds and notes
      secured by a lien on securities or other negotiable paper, and
      stock trust certificates that are similar in character to
      collateral trust bonds;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} income bonds --- bonds that are a lien on a
      company's revenue alone, or bonds that, while being a lien on the
      company's property and franchises, provide for payments of
      interest only if the interest is earned;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} convertible bonds --- bonds that may be converted
      into capital stock of the company, according to the agreement
      under which they are issued; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} miscellaneous obligations --- all other long-term
      debt obligations maturing more than one year from the date of the
      balance sheet.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Each of the classes listed in subsection (3) shall
    be divided into subclasses according to

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} differences in security given therefor;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} rates of interest;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} interest dates; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} dates of maturity.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Long term debt securities are considered to be
    issued when they have been sold to a [bona fide]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} purchaser for valuable consideration and when the
    purchaser holds them free from all control by the accounting
    company.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} All long-term debt securities that are issued and
    not reacquired and are held by or for the company are considered to
    be outstanding.

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} Long-term debt that is to be repaid in a foreign
    currency shall be included in this account in accordance with
    generally accepted accounting practice.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [221]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Company Long-term Debt Owned*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the par value of
    long-term debt actually issued or assumed by a company and
    reacquired by it and not retired or cancelled, but shall not include
    long-term debt held in sinking funds or miscellaneous special funds.
    (*See* section 74 of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the long-term debt referred to in
    subsection (1) is to be repaid in a foreign currency, for purposes
    of that subsection par value means the par value in the foreign
    currency converted to the Canadian dollar equivalent.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} This account shall show

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} total par value unpledged; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} total par value pledged.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [248]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Advances from Affiliated Companies*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include all amounts owed to
    affiliated companies and not subject to settlement within one year
    and, without limiting the generality of the foregoing, shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the par value of bonds, debentures and long-term
      notes payable and issued to affiliated companies;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the par value of non-negotiable notes issued to
      affiliated companies;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the par value of matured long-term bonds,
      debentures or notes payable where such bonds, debentures or notes
      of the company are held by affiliated companies, where there is no
      agreement for an extension as to time of payment but the
      collection of the principal is not enforced;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} credit balances in open accounts with affiliated
      companies, other than credit balances in current accounts; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} interest accrued on any of the items referred to
      in paragraphs (a) to (d) when the interest is not subject to
      current settlement.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Separate subaccounts shall be kept for each of the
    items listed in subsection (1). (*See* section 80 of these
    Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [249]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Long-term Debt*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include amounts payable more than one year from the
  date of the balance sheet where such amounts have not been provided
  for elsewhere and loans payable authorized by subsection 76(2) to be
  included in this account.

###### [Current Liabilities]{.HTitleText4} {#current-liabilities .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [250]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Loans and Notes Payable*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the balances
    representing a company's outstanding obligations in the form of
    loans and notes payable or other similar evidences of indebtedness
    payable on demand or within a time not exceeding one year from the
    date of the balance sheet.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be maintained in such form as to
    show separately the amounts of indebtedness secured by collateral.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [251]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accounts Payable and Accrued*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of vouchers and payrolls or accounts
      unpaid on the date of the balance sheet;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outstanding drafts drawn by agents;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} unpaid rents under leases or agreements;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} taxes collected from employees and others for the
      account of taxing agencies; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} other items of the nature of demand liabilities
      not included in account 250 (Loans and Notes Payable), account 252
      (Accounts Payable --- Affiliated Companies), account 257 (Interest
      Payable and Accrued) and account 259 (Other Current Liabilities).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall also include a company's
    estimated accrued liabilities with respect to accounts payable.
    (*See* sections 77 and 78 of these Regulations)

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company's estimates of liabilities for injuries
    to persons and for loss and damage claims shall not be included in
    this account but shall be credited to account 259 (Other Current
    Liabilities) or account 279 (Other Deferred Credits), as applicable.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [252]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accounts Payable --- Affiliated Companies*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include all amounts owed to affiliated companies
  and subject to settlement within one year from the date of the balance
  sheet and, without limiting the generality of the foregoing, shall
  include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} advances;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} credit balances and open accounts;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} long-term debt due within one year;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} joint revenue payable;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} interest on any of the above;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} dividends; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} miscellaneous amounts.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [253]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Dividends Payable*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include dividends declared on capital stock and not
  yet paid by a company other than dividends payable to affiliated
  companies and recorded in account 252.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [254]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Customers' Security Deposits*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include all amounts deposited with a company by
  customers as security for payment of debts.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [256]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Taxes Accrued*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be credited with the accruals of
    all taxes that are currently payable to federal, provincial or other
    governmental authorities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The accruals referred to in subsection (1) may be
    based upon estimates, provided that the estimates shall be adjusted
    to reflect in this account at all times the estimates of the company
    as to its unpaid liability for each of the several classes of taxes
    that has not been finally settled.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} All tax payments for which accruals have been made
    shall be debited to this account.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The records supporting the entries in this account
    shall be kept to show separately, by classes of taxes, the amount of
    the tax accruals for the current year and the adjustments of
    accruals for prior years.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [257]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Interest Payable and Accrued*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall be credited with interest due or accrued on

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notes payable,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bank overdrafts and loans, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} long-term debt and other obligations,

  other than such interest payable to affiliated companies.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [258]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Long-term Debt Due Within One Year*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of long-term debt and any premiums
    thereon that will become payable within one year from the date of
    the balance sheet,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of matured and unpaid long-term debt and
    any premiums thereon for which there has been no specific agreement
    for extension of the time of payment, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amount of long-term debt called for redemption
    but not presented to the company or its agents for payment,

  other than long-term debt payable to affiliated companies.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [259]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Current Liabilities*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include all contractor's hold backs and all other
  current liabilities not provided for elsewhere, including the
  company's estimate of current liabilities in respect of injuries to
  persons and loss and damage claims.

###### [Deferred Credits and Appropriations]{.HTitleText4} {#deferred-credits-and-appropriations .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [270]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Unamortized Debt Premium and Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the unamortized premiums
    received from the issuance of long-term debt and shall include the
    total of the credit balances in the subaccounts maintained for each
    series of each class of long-term debt in accordance with
    section 71.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amounts recorded in this account shall be
    amortized in accordance with section 72 or 73, as applicable.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [275]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Cost and Maintenance Equalization*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the undistributed debit or credit resulting from a
    company following a method of equalizing its gas cost through the
    year because of seasonal variations in unit gas prices; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ledger balances representing reserves created
    by debits to operations for maintenance expenses in accordance with
    section 47.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [276]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Accumulated Deferred Income Taxes*
  :::
  :::::

  Where a company does not follow the taxes payable basis of accounting
  for income taxes, this account shall include the amounts by which a
  company's provision for income taxes for a particular year differs
  from the amount of income taxes payable for that year.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [278]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Contributions and Grants*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include non-refundable
    contributions or grants in cash, services or property from
    governments or government agencies, corporations, individuals and
    others for contributions in aid of construction.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Amounts included in this account shall not be
    transferred to any other account without the prior approval of the
    Commission.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be maintained in a manner that
    will furnish complete information respecting the purpose of each
    contribution or grant.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [279]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Deferred Credits*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} credit items that cannot be disposed of until
    further information is received;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} undistributed balances in clearing accounts;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} variances in estimated and actual foreign exchange
    expenditures arising from foreign currency service of debt
    securities issued by a company and variances between estimated and
    actual rates of foreign exchange realized from repurchases of debt
    securities issued by the company where such a deferral of foreign
    exchange variances has been directed by the Commission to facilitate
    consideration of disposition at a later date;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} material gain on extraordinary retirement that is
    authorized by the Commission to be transferred from accumulated
    depreciation or accumulated amortization accounts to this account,
    in accordance with subsection 40(3) which gain shall be amortized as
    directed by the Commission; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} credit items of a deferred nature, not provided for
    elsewhere, that are to be amortized over future periods.

###### [Appropriations]{.HTitleText4} {#appropriations .SchedHeadL4}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [290]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Insurance Appropriations*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the credit balances
    representing insurance appropriations concurrently debited to
    expenses to cover self-carried risks on fire, fidelity, vehicle,
    boiler, casualty, burglary, other insurance, and re-insurance
    recoveries from insurance companies. (*See* sections 61 and 62 of
    these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} To the extent that a loss or damage sustained is
    covered by this account, the amount of the loss or damage shall be
    debited to this account.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Separate subaccounts shall be kept for each kind of
    insurance reserve created.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [291]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Welfare and Pension Appropriations*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the credit balances representing the liability of
      the company for amounts contributed by employees and by the
      company through debits to expenses or such other accounts as are
      approved by the Commission for

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} pensions,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} accident and death benefits,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} savings,

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} relief, and

      - [(v)]{.lawlabel} hospital or other provident purposes,

    whether or not a special fund has been established to meet such
    liabilities (*See* section 62 and account 123 (Miscellaneous Special
    Funds) of these Regulations); and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the credit balances representing the liability of
      the company for amounts provided by debits to expenses in respect
      of deferred compensation under an employees' profit-sharing plan.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Separate subaccounts shall be kept for each kind of
    appropriation created pursuant to subsection (1).

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Disbursements shall be debited to this account and
    any excess of such disbursements over the amounts appropriated shall
    be transferred to account 724 (Injuries and Damages), or to
    account 725 (Employee Benefits), as applicable.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Current amounts payable relative to funded welfare,
    pension and other plans with insurance companies or trustees shall
    be transferred to account 251 (Accounts Payable and Accrued).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [293]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Appropriations*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall be credited with all appropriations or allowances
  that may not be included in account 145 (Allowance for Doubtful
  Accounts), account 290 (Insurance Appropriations), account 291
  (Welfare and Pension Appropriations) or account 126 (Allowance for
  Loss in Value of Investments), including estimated liabilities for
  injuries to persons and loss and damage claims not includible in
  account 259 (Other Current Liabilities).

## [SCHEDULE II]{.scheduleLabel}[(ss. 6, 8 and 14)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-876313 .scheduleLabel}
:::

Incomes Taxes
</div>

<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">307</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Revenue from Gas Plant Leased to Others
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">308</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Rent for Gas Plant Leased from Others
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">310</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Revenue from Other Plant
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">311</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Expenses of Other Plant
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">312</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Non-Operating Revenue
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">313</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Non-Operating Expense
</div>
</div></li>
