#### [Text Pertaining to Income Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#text-pertaining-to-income-accounts .SchedHeadL2}

##### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#general .SchedHeadL3}

The balance of all income accounts shall be closed into retained
earnings account 350 (Balance Transferred from Income) at the end of
each fiscal year.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [300]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Operating Revenue*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the total revenues derived from operations
  recorded in the accounts set out in Schedule V to these Regulations.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [301]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Operating Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the total expenses of operations recorded
  in the accounts set out in Schedule VI to these Regulations.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [302]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Maintenance Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the total maintenance expenses recorded in
  the accounts set out in Schedule VII to these Regulations.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [303]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Depreciation*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include debits for the
    depreciation of plant included in

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} account 100 (Gas Plant in Service);

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others); and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} account 102 (Gas Plant Held for Future Use).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsidiary records shall be maintained to show
    separately the depreciation expense for each plant account or group
    of plant accounts that perform similar functions. (*See* sections 49
    to 59 of these Regulations inclusive and the text of account 105)

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} This account shall also include debits for the
    depreciation of Improvements to Facilities Leased from Others, where
    it is inappropriate to amortize the amounts of such improvements
    over the life of the lease by debit to account 304 (Amortization).
    (*See* text of account 107)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [304]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Amortization*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include debits for the
    amortization of plant included in

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} account 100 (Gas Plant in Service),

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others), and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} account 102 (Gas Plant Held for Future Use)

    when a change from depreciation accounting to amortization
    accounting has been approved by the Commission. (*See* section 59 of
    these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall also include debits for the
    amortization of amounts recorded in account 171 (Extraordinary Plant
    Losses), when approval or direction of the Commission has been
    received. (*See* text of account 171)

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} This account shall also include debits for the
    amortization of Improvements to Facilities Leased from Others.
    (*See* text of account 107)

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsidiary records shall be maintained to show
    separately the amortization expense for each plant account or group
    of plant accounts that perform similar functions.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} This account shall also include credits for the
    amortization of amounts recorded in account 278 (Contributions and
    Grants), when approval or direction of the Commission has been
    received. (*See* text of account 278)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [305]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Municipal and Other Taxes*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} taxes levied by governments and municipalities
      relating to plant, operations and privileges, whether based upon
      valuation of the plant, quantity of gas transmitted, length of
      pipeline operated or owned, or other bases;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} instalment payments covering assessments by
      governmental authorities for public improvements, and the
      write-off of lump sum payments originally debited to account 176
      (Public Improvements); and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} other taxes not provided for elsewhere.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Taxes on new lines under construction or on plant
    acquired for the extension of existing lines or on additions or
    replacements before the facilities are opened for commercial
    operations or on plant acquired before it becomes available for
    service shall be debited to the appropriate gas plant accounts.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [306]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Income Taxes*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include provision for federal, provincial or other
  government income taxes levied on the basis of a company's operations
  for the fiscal period, except income taxes chargeable to account 353
  (Income Tax Applicable to Retained Earnings Adjustments).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be subdivided as follows:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} current income taxes applicable to income before
      extraordinary items;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} current income taxes and reductions of current
      income taxes applicable to extraordinary items; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} deferred income taxes.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [307]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Revenue from Gas Plant Leased to Others*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include revenues from gas plant that is included in
  account 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others) and that constitutes a
  distinct operating unit or system leased by the company to others.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [308]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Rent for Gas Plant Leased from Others*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include rent for gas plant that is leased by the
  company from others and that constitutes a distinct operating unit or
  system.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [310]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Revenue from Other Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the total revenues from
    the operation of plant that is included in account 110 (Other
    Plant).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Revenues from Other
    Plant to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Revenue from commercial power plants
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Revenue from lands and buildings not used in gas operations
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Revenue from lands and plant acquired and held in anticipation of an
    indefinite future use
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Revenue from mineral and timber lands
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [311]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Expense of Other Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all expenses, including depreciation but
      excluding income taxes, arising from the operation of other plant
      the cost of which is included in account 110 (Other Plant); and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all expenses, other than depreciation, of gas
      plant leased to others as operating units or systems while the
      lessee has exclusive possession.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Expense of Other
    Plant to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expense of commercial power plants
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expense of lands and buildings not used in gas operations
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expense of lands and plant acquired in anticipation of an indefinite
    future use
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expense of mineral and timber lands
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [312]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Non-Operating Revenue*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include revenues that are from commercial
  activities carried on within the corporate structure of the company
  and that may not be included in account 300 (Operating Revenue).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [313]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Non-Operating Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include expenses from commercial activities carried
  on within the corporate structure of the company that may not be
  included in account 301 (Operating Expense) or account 302
  (Maintenance Expense).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [314]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Income from Investments*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} interest and dividends from investments recorded
      in account 121 (Other Investments) and account 132 (Temporary Cash
      Investments);

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} interest on bank balances, open accounts, special
      deposits and other analogous items, where the interest is the
      property of the company; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the amounts involved are not material,
      gains and losses on *sale*, or amounts required to provide
      allowances for the decrease in market value of temporary cash
      investments. (*See* sections 64 and 65 of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the option of the company, there may be included
    in this account the applicable portion of discount or premium on any
    investments recorded in accounts 121 and 132. (*See* section 63 of
    these Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [315]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Income from Affiliated Companies*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include income from the
    investment of a company in shares, securities and other types of
    indebtedness that are issued or assumed by affiliated companies and
    in respect of which the income is the property of the accounting
    company, whether such shares, securities and other types of
    indebtedness are owned by the accounting company and held in its
    treasury or deposited in trust, or are otherwise controlled. (*See*
    section 80 of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amounts included in this account shall be
    divided to show separately the types of income as follows:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} dividends;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} interest; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} other income.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Income from affiliated companies shall not be
    credited to this account until such time as it is recorded on the
    books of the affiliated company.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} At the option of the company, there may be included
    in this account the applicable portion of discount or premium on any
    investments recorded in account 120. (*See* section 63 of these
    Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [316]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Income from Sinking and Other Funds*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the income accrued on cash, securities and other
      assets, not including debt securities issued or assumed by the
      company, recorded in account 122 (Sinking Funds) and account 123
      (Miscellaneous Special Funds) (*See* section 62 of these
      Regulations); and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} immaterial gains realized and losses sustained in
      the sales of securities recorded in account 122 (Sinking Funds)
      and account 123 (Miscellaneous Special Funds). (*See* section 62
      of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the option of the company, there may be included
    in this account the applicable portion of the discount or premium on
    any securities recorded in accounts 122 and 123. (*See* section 63
    of these Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [317]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gain on Foreign Exchange*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include gains on foreign exchange during the fiscal
  period, including realized gains as well as gains owing to the
  conversion to the Canadian dollar equivalent, on assets and
  liabilities held in foreign currencies.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [319]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Income*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} gains realized from the sale of materials and
    supplies not purchased for resale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} immaterial gains realized from reacquisition at
    less than net book value of the debt securities issued by the
    company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} immaterial gains arising from the sale of land,
    other plant or investments carried in account 120 (Investments in
    Affiliated Companies) and account 121 (Other Investments); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all other income items not provided for elsewhere.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [320]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Interest on Long-Term Debt*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include current accruals of
    interest on all classes of long-term debt included in account 220
    (Long-Term Debt), account 249 (Other Long-Term Debt) and account 258
    (Long-Term Debt Due Within One Year), less current accruals on the
    debt included in account 221 (Company Long-Term Debt Owned).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be maintained to show separately
    the interest on each class of long-term debt.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [321]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Amortization of Debt Discount, Premium and Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be debited or credited, as
    applicable, during each fiscal period, with the proportion of the
    discount, premium and expense on long-term debt obligations as may
    be applicable to the period.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The proportion referred to in subsection (1) shall
    be determined in accordance with section 71, 72 or 73 as applicable.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [322]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Interest on Amounts Due Affiliated Companies*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include current accruals of interest of all classes
  of debt included in account 248 (Advances from Affiliated Companies)
  or account 252 (Accounts Payable --- Affiliated Companies).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [323]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Interest*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all interest except that chargeable to account 320
    (Interest on Long Term Debt) or account 322 (Interest on Amounts Due
    Affiliated Companies); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all discounts, premiums and expenses on short term
    notes issued by the company and maturing in one year or less from
    the date of the balance sheet.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [324]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Allowance for Funds Used During Construction* (*Credit*)
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall be credited with amounts concurrently debited to
  account 497 (Allowance for Funds Used During Construction). (*See*
  section 27 of these Regulations and account 497)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [325]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Loss on Foreign Exchange*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include losses on foreign exchange during the
  fiscal period, including realized losses as well as losses owing to
  the conversion to the Canadian dollar equivalent, on assets and
  liabilities held in foreign currencies.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [326]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Provision for Loss in Valuation of Investments*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be debited with amounts
    concurrently credited to account 126 (Allowance for Loss in
    Valuation of Investments), in respect of immaterial reductions in
    the value of investments carried in account 120 (Investments in
    Affiliated Companies) or account 121 (Other Investments). (*See*
    text of account 126 and section 67 of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall also include a company's share
    of the losses or subsequent adjustments thereof described in
    section 68, unless such losses or adjustments are material.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [329]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Income Deductions*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} immaterial losses through reacquisitions at greater
    than net book value of debt securities issued by the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} immaterial losses arising from the sale of land,
    other plant or investments carried in account 120 (Investments in
    Affiliated Companies), and account 121 (Other Investments); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all other income deductions not provided for
    elsewhere.

#### [Extraordinary Items]{.HTitleText2} {#extraordinary-items .SchedHeadL2}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [331]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Extraordinary Income*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise directed by the Commission in
    particular cases, a company shall include in this account all
    material gains constituting "Extraordinary Items". (*See*
    subsections 8(2) and (4) of these Regulations.)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Extraordinary
    Income to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on extraordinary retirement of depreciable plant (<em>See</em>
    section 40)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on sale or retirement of depreciable Other Plant (<em>See</em>
    section 43)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on sale or retirement of land (<em>See</em> section 44)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on sale of assets included in Sinking Funds or Miscellaneous
    Special Funds (<em>See</em> section 62)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on sale of Temporary Cash Investments (<em>See</em> section 64)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on sale of Investments in Affiliated Companies or Other Investments
    (<em>See</em> section 66)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Adjustment to account 126 as described in subsection 68(2) of these
    Regulations
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on redemption of long-term debt (<em>See</em> section 73)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gain on reacquisition of long-term debt (<em>See</em> section 74)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [341]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Extraordinary Income Deductions*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} A company shall include in this account all
    material losses constituting "Extraordinary Items". (*See*
    subsections 8(2) and (4) of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative list of Items of extraordinary
    deductions to be included in the account. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Loss on an extraordinary retirement of depreciable plant (<em>See</em>
    section 40)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Loss on sale or retirement of depreciable Other Plant (<em>See</em>
    section 43)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Loss on sale or retirement of land (<em>See</em> section 44)
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>

## [SCHEDULE III]{.scheduleLabel}[(ss. 6, 9, 14 and item 212 of Sch. I)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-876494 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [Index of Accounts]{.HTitleText1} {#index-of-accounts-2 .SchedHeadL1}

<figure>
<ul>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">350</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Balance Transferred from Income
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">351</span> 
</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Prior Period Adjustments
</div>
</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">
<div class="listItemLabel">
<span class="lawlabel">352</span>

<div class="listItemText2">
Miscellaneous Retained Earnings Adjustments
</div>

</div>
</div></li>

Dividend Appropriations
</div>

<figcaption><p><span class="HTitleText2">Retained Earnings
Accounts</span></p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Text Pertaining to Retained Earnings Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#text-pertaining-to-retained-earnings-accounts .SchedHeadL2}

##### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#general-1 .SchedHeadL3}

The balance in all retained earnings accounts shall be closed to balance
sheet account 212 (Retained Earnings) at the end of such calendar year
or other fiscal year as is approved by the Commission for the closing of
the company's accounts.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [350]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Balance Transferred from Income*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall show the debit or credit balances brought forward
  from the income accounts for the fiscal year.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [351]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Prior Period Adjustments*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise directed by the Commission, a
    company shall include in this account all material adjustments to
    the income of prior fiscal years when such adjustments have all four
    of the characteristics set out in subsection 78(1).

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Prior Period
    Adjustments to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Adjustments or settlements of income taxes
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Adjustments or settlements of claims resulting from litigation
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [352]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Miscellaneous Retained Earnings Adjustments*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include adjustments to retained
    earnings not provided for elsewhere.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not use this account without
    obtaining the prior approval of the Commission.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [353]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Income Tax Applicable to Retained Earnings Adjustments*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the estimated federal, provincial or other
  government income taxes (debit or credit) assignable to the aggregate
  of items, of both taxable income and deductions from taxable income,
  that, for accounting purposes, are recorded in retaining earnings
  accounts.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [357]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Dividend Appropriations*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the amount of dividends
    declared out of retained earnings during the year on all classes of
    outstanding shares.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsidiary records shall be maintained showing
    separately the dividends declared on each class of shares.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} When dividends are paid in other than money,
    complete details of each transaction shall be maintained.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Dividends declared out of contributed surplus shall
    be debited to account 210 (Contributed Surplus).

::: HistoricalNote

#### [Text Pertaining to Plant Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#text-pertaining-to-plant-accounts .SchedHeadL2}

##### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#general-2 .SchedHeadL3}

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} The total of the balances in the plant accounts shall
  equal the total of the balance sheet accounts 100 (Gas Plant in
  Service), 101 (Gas Plant Leased to Others), 102 (Gas Plant Held for
  Future Use) and 107 (Improvements to Facilities Leased from Others).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the same account title is used for more than
  one group of plant accounts, the account text instructions are given
  in detail for the accounts under the gathering plant group.

##### [Intangible Plant]{.HTitleText3} {#intangible-plant .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [401]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Franchises and Consents*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} amounts paid to federal, provincial or other
      governmental authorities in consideration for franchises, consents
      or certificates running in perpetuity or for a specified term of
      more than one year; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} expenses incidental to procuring the franchises,
      consents or certificates of permission and approval.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a franchise, consent or certificate is
    acquired by assignment, the amount paid to the assignor shall be
    debited to this account.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a franchise, consent or certificate is
    retired or expires, the book cost shall be credited to this account.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a franchise, consent or certificate referred
    to in subsection (3) is depreciable, the book cost shall be debited
    to account 105 (Accumulated Depreciation --- Gas Plant) or
    account 106 (Accumulated Amortization --- Gas Plant), as applicable.
    (*See* section 40 and subsection 51(2) of these Regulations)

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the franchise, consent or certificate
    referred to in subsection (3) is not depreciable and the gain or
    loss from the retirement or expiration is material, the company
    shall inform the Regulator and, unless otherwise directed by the
    Commission, shall transfer the amount of the gain or loss to account
    331 (Extraordinary Income) or account 341 (Extraordinary Income
    Deductions), as applicable.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where the franchise, consent or certificate
    referred to in subsection (3) is not depreciable and the gain or
    loss from the retirement or expiration is not material, the gain or
    loss shall be transferred to account 319 (Other Income) or
    account 329 (Other Income Deductions), as applicable.

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} Assessments with respect to franchises, consents or
    certificates shall not be included in this account.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [402]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Intangible Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of patent
    rights, allowances, privileges and other intangible plant necessary
    or valuable in the operation of a company's gas pipeline service and
    not specifically provided for elsewhere.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} When any item included in this account is retired
    or expires, the cost shall be credited to this account.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the item referred to in subsection (2) is
    depreciable, the cost shall be debited to account 105 (Accumulated
    Depreciation --- Gas Plant) or account 106 (Accumulated Amortization
    --- Gas Plant), as applicable. (*See* section 40 and
    subsection 51(2) of these Regulations)

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the item referred to in subsection (2) is not
    depreciable and the gain or loss from the retirement or expiration
    is material, the company shall inform the Regulator and, unless
    otherwise directed by the Commission, shall transfer the amount of
    the gain or loss to account 331 (Extraordinary Income) or account
    341 (Extraordinary Income Deductions), as applicable.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the item referred to in subsection (2) is not
    depreciable and the gain or loss from the retirement or expiration
    is not material, the gain or loss shall be transferred to
    account 319 (Other Income) or account 329 (Other Income Deductions),
    as applicable.

##### [Gathering Plant]{.HTitleText3} {#gathering-plant .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [410]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Land*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of land owned in fee simple and used for
      the construction and operation of pipelines; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs of removing the property of others from
      the land and of relocating such property.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cost of land not used in gas pipeline service
    or for which there is no definite plan for use in pipeline service
    shall be debited to account 110 (Other Plant).

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the purchase of land for gas pipeline service
    requires the purchase of more land than is needed for such purposes,
    the charge to the specific land account shall be based upon the cost
    of the land purchased, less the fair market value of that portion of
    land that may not be included in account 100, 101 or 102.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The portion of the cost of land referred to in
    subsection (3) that may not be included in account 100, 101 or 102
    shall be debited to account 110 (Other Plant).

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} The cost of acquiring options to purchase land for
    use in contemplated projects shall be debited to account 172
    (Preliminary Survey and Investigation Charges) pending a
    determination as to whether or not to proceed with such projects.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} The costs of acquiring options to purchase land for
    use in current construction projects shall be debited to account 115
    (Gas Plant Under Construction).

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} Net proceeds from the sale of timber, minerals or
    improvements that were part of the land cost shall be credited to
    this account.

  - [(8)]{.lawlabel} Any excess of the net proceeds referred to in
    subsection (7) over the original cost of the timber, minerals or
    improvements included in the land cost shall be credited to account
    319 (Other Income) if the excess is not material or, if material, to
    account 331 (Extraordinary Income) after Commission approval has
    been obtained.

  - [(9)]{.lawlabel} When land is acquired together with buildings, the
    cost shall be fairly apportioned between land and buildings and
    accounted for accordingly unless the plan of acquisition
    contemplates the immediate removal of the buildings, in which case
    the entire cost shall be debited to this account and any salvage
    value from the buildings shall be credited to this account.

  - [(10)]{.lawlabel} Gains or losses arising from the sale of land
    shall be debited or credited to the appropriate income account.
    (*See* section 44 of these Regulations)

  - [(11)]{.lawlabel} The cost of clearing, levelling or grading land,
    both before and after the construction of facilities thereon, shall,
    if directly related to such facilities, be included in the plant
    accounts provided for the cost of the facilities constructed.

  - [(12)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Land to be
    included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [411]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Land Rights*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include amounts paid for land
    rights or easements and the cost incurred in connection with
    obtaining those land rights.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Temporary land rights or easements used for
    construction purposes only and the cost of clearing, levelling or
    grading land for which the company has acquired land rights both
    before and after the construction of facilities thereon, but
    directly related to such facilities, shall be included in the plant
    account provided for the facilities constructed.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Net proceeds from the sale of timber, minerals or
    improvements that were part of the cost of land rights shall be
    credited to this account.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any excess of the net proceeds referred to in
    subsection (3) over the original cost of the timber, minerals or
    improvements included in the cost of the land rights shall be
    credited to account 319 (Other Income) if the excess is not material
    or, if material, to account 331 (Extraordinary Income) after
    Commission approval has been obtained.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Land Rights to be
    included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

      improvements to such structures or facilities and the cost of
      clearing, levelling or grading land both before and after

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [415]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gathering Lines*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of installed line pipe and line pipe
      fittings laid in the construction of gathering lines between the
      delivery meters of separate gas fields and the meters recording
      delivery to the transmission system; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of re-opening the trench and
      back-filling subsequent to original construction for the purpose
      of applying the original protection and casing.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Gathering Lines to
    be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

      facilities or function referred to in paragraph (a), the cost of

      clearing, levelling or grading land both before and after
      construction of the structures or facilities.

    Plant to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  ::: listItemText2
  *Compressor Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of compressor
    and associated equipment used in connection with the receipt,
    processing and return of gas processed for the removal of gasoline,
    butane, propane or other saleable products.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Compressor
    Equipment to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 416(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [427]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Measuring and Regulating Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of meters,
    gauges and other equipment used in measuring or regulating gas
    received or returned from processing for the removal of gasoline,
    butane, propane or other saleable products.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Measuring and
    Regulating Equipment to be included. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 417(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [428]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2

  :::
  :::::

  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Products Extraction Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of equipment used in processing
  gas and refining gasoline, butane, propane and other saleable products
  extracted from gas, not provided for elsewhere.

    </div>

    </div></li>

    </div>

    </div>
    </div></li>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [455]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Field Lines*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the costs of installed
    pipelines used in conveying gas from the point of connection with
    gathering or transmission lines to underground storage wells and
    from underground storage wells to the point where the gas enters the
    transmission system.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Field Lines to be
    included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 415(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [456]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Compressor Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of compressor
    equipment used to deliver gas to underground storage or to withdraw
    gas from underground storage for delivery to the transmission
    system.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Representative List of Items of Compressor
    Equipment to be included. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 416(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [457]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Measuring and Regulating Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of meters,
    gauges and other equipment used in measuring or regulating
    deliveries of gas to underground storage and withdrawals of gas from
    underground storage.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Measuring and
    Regulating Equipment to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 417(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [458]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Base Pressure Gas*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include gas in underground storage that is required
  as a base pressure for the operation of underground storage areas,
  valued at average cost on a consistent basis. (*See* account 152, Gas
  Stored Underground --- Available for Sale)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [459]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Underground Storage Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of equipment used in connection
  with underground storage of gas and not provided for elsewhere in
  these accounts.

##### [Transmission Plant]{.HTitleText3} {#transmission-plant .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [460]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Land*
  :::
  :::::

  For the text of this account, *see* account 410.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [461]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Land Rights*
  :::
  :::::

  For the text of this account, *see* account 411.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [462]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Compressor Structures and Improvements*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of structures and related facilities
      used in connection with the compressor function of gas
      transmission operations; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where directly related to the structures,
      facilities or function referred to in paragraph (a), the cost of
      improvements to such structures or facilities and the cost of
      clearing, levelling or grading land both before and after
      construction of the structures or facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Compressor
    Structures and Improvements to be included*. (*See* section 14 of
    these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 412(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [463]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Measuring and Regulating Structures and Improvements*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of structures and related facilities
      used in connection with the measuring and regulating function of
      gas transmission operations; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where directly related to the structures,
      facilities or functions referred to in paragraph (a), the cost of
      improvements to such structures or facilities and the cost of
      clearing, levelling or grading land both before and after
      construction of the structures or facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Measuring and
    Regulating Structures and Improvements to be included*. (*See*
    section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 413(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [464]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Structures and Improvements*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of structures and related facilities
      used in gas transmission operations not provided for elsewhere;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where directly related to the structures,
      facilities or function referred to in paragraph (a), the cost of
      improvements to such structures or facilities and the cost of
      clearing, levelling or grading land both before and after
      construction of the structures or facilities; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cost of improvements to non-owned property.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Other Structures
    and Improvements to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 414(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [465]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Mains*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of installed line pipe and line pipe
      fittings laid in the construction of gas transmission lines
      between the discharge valve of a station and the suction valve of
      the next station, including pipe from receiving meters to delivery
      meters; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of re-opening the trench and
      back-filling, subsequent to the original construction, for the
      purpose of applying the original protection and casing.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Mains to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 415(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [466]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Compressor Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of compressors
    and associated equipment used in connection with gas transmission
    operations.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Compressor
    Equipment to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 416(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [467]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Measuring and Regulating Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of meters,
    gauges and other equipment used in measuring or regulating gas in
    connection with gas transmission operations.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Measuring and
    Regulating Equipment to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 417(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [468]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Communication Structures and Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of telephone,
    telegraph, radio and other communication structures and equipment
    used wholly or predominantly in connection with gas transmission
    operations.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Communication
    Structures and Equipment to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [488]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Communication Structures and Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of telegraph,
    telephone, radio and other communication structures and equipment
    used wholly or predominantly in the general operations of the
    company.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Communication
    Structures and Equipment to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 468(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [489]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Equipment*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of equipment used in gas pipeline
  operations not provided for elsewhere.

##### [Undistributed Plant]{.HTitleText3} {#undistributed-plant .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [496]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Unclassified Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of gas plant in service pending
  analyses and distribution to the appropriate plant account.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [497]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Allowance for Funds Used During Construction*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not, without the approval of the
    Commission, charge an allowance for funds used during construction
    (in this section referred to as an "allowance").

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The rate used to determine the amount of an
    allowance shall be based on a reasonable allowance for the use of
    funds expended during the construction period, whether or not such
    funds have been borrowed.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where an allowance is capitalized, the relevant
    amounts shall be debited to this account and concurrently credited
    to account 324 (Allowance for Funds Used During Construction
    (Credit)).

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} An allowance capitalized prior to the coming into
    force of these Regulations shall be included in this account, except
    where it has been prorated over the appropriate plant accounts.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company may, at its option, close out this
    account by prorating the allowance to the plant accounts (*See*
    section 27 of these Regulations).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [498]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Overhead Charged to Construction*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include that portion of a
    company's overhead expenditure that is attributable to the
    acquisition of plant, such portion being calculated on the basis of
    actual costs equitably allocated to each job or unit.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may, at its option, close out this
    account by prorating the amounts recorded therein to the appropriate
    plant account. (*See* section 26 of these Regulations)

::: HistoricalNote

## [SCHEDULE V]{.scheduleLabel}[(ss. 6, 11, 14 and item 300 of Sch. II)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-877300 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [Index of Accounts]{.HTitleText1} {#index-of-accounts-4 .SchedHeadL1}

<figure>
<ul>

<div class="listItemLabel">

</div>

</div>
<div class="listItemText2">
Foreign Sales

</div></li>
<li><div class="listItemBlock1">

</div>

Other Sales

Miscellaneous Operating Revenue
</div>
</div></li>
</ul>
<figcaption><p><span class="HTitleText2">Operation Revenue
Accounts</span></p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Text Pertaining to Operating Revenue Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#text-pertaining-to-operating-revenue-accounts .SchedHeadL2}

##### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#general-3 .SchedHeadL3}

The total of the balances in the operating revenue accounts shall be
included in income account 300 (Operating Revenue).

##### [Operating Revenue]{.HTitleText3} {#operating-revenue .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [500]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Canadian Sales*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include revenue from the sale of gas to utilities
  and other companies domiciled in Canada

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [510]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Foreign Sales*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include revenue from the sale of gas to utilities
  and other companies domiciled outside Canada.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [529]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Sales*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include revenue from the sale of gas not provided
  for elsewhere.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [550]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Sales of Products Extracted from Gas*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include revenues from the sale
    of natural gas, gasoline, butane, propane and other products
    extracted from gas.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be subdivided as follows:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} natural gasoline sales;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} butane sales;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} propane sales; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} other extracted products sales.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [551]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Revenue from Natural Gas Processed by Others*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the revenue derived from royalties and
  permits or from any other basis of settlement arising from permission
  granted to others for the right to remove products from natural gas.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [570]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Transportation and Storage of Gas of Others*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the revenue from transporting and storing
  non-owned gas in the company's system.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [575]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Rent from Gas Plant*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include rent revenues from the use by others of
  assets included in account 100 (Gas Plant in Service).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [579]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Miscellaneous Operating Revenue*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} revenues earned in consideration of the performance
    of exchange gas services; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} other miscellaneous operating revenue not provided
    for elsewhere.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-50, s. 13(F)](#1239674-1240656){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1239674-1240656.html"
  role="button"}

#### [Text Pertaining to Operating Expense Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#text-pertaining-to-operating-expense-accounts .SchedHeadL2}

##### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#general-4 .SchedHeadL3}

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} The total of the balances in the operating expense
  accounts is included in income account 301 (Operating Expenses).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the same account title is used in more than one
  group of operating expense accounts, the account text instructions are
  given in detail for the accounts under the gathering group.

##### [Gathering Operation]{.HTitleText3} {#gathering-operation .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [610]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Supervision*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred at a division, district or similar
    field office in the general supervision of the operation of the
    gathering system facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Supervision to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Salaries and wages of superintendents, engineers, clerks, stenographers
    and other field office employees
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Travelling, subsistence, automotive and incidental expenses of employees
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Office supplies and expenses such as stationery, postage, maps,
    telephone, telegraph, light, heat, water
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Rents, janitor services and supplies, maintenance of office buildings
    and equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [612]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gathering of Gas by Others*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of gathering charges paid to
  others where the charges are separate and not included as part of the
  delivered price of gas.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [615]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Gas used or lost in purification operations
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [619]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Gathering Operations*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of labour, supplies and expenses
  that are incurred in the operation of the gathering system facilities
  and that are not provided for elsewhere.

##### [Products Extraction Operation]{.HTitleText3} {#products-extraction-operation .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [620]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Supervision*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the general supervision of the
    operation of product extraction and refining facilities

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Supervision to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 610(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [621]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Extraction and Refining*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of labour, supplies and expenses
      incurred in the operation of products extraction and refining
      facilities; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct supervision of specific activities such as
      power plant, extraction and refining units, laboratory and
      warehouse facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Extraction and
    Refining to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Direct supervision
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Operating, cleaning, lubricating machinery, valves, gauges, instruments
    and other equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>

    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Inspecting and testing instruments and other equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Reading meters, gauges and other instruments, changing charts, gauge
    glasses
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Taking and testing water samples, gas samples and other similar
    laboratory services
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Tending plant grounds, removing snow
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Producing electricity, steam, pumping cooling water
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Preparing logs, reports and other data
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Supplies such as amine, dehydration agents, water treatment and other
    chemicals, absorption oil, lubricants, wiping rags, charts, light bulbs,
    gauge glasses, cleaning and washroom supplies, first aid supplies,
    landscaping supplies, gas used or lost in operations, hand tools and
    equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expenses such as use of automotive, trucking, aircraft and heavy work
    equipment, transportation of supplies, light, heat, power, water,
    telephone, telegraph, rents employee travelling and subsistence,
    employee housing.
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [622]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Processing by Others*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of gas shrinkage, gas consumed for
  fuel, royalties and other expenses in connection with the processing
  of gas of the company by others for extraction of saleable products.

##### [Gas Supply Accounts]{.HTitleText3} {#gas-supply-accounts .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [623]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Purchases*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost, at point of receipt by the
  company, of all gas purchased.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [626]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Exchange Gas*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of minor transactions, this
    account shall include the debits or credits for the cost of gas in
    unbalanced exchange transactions with another party when the
    delivery or receipt of the gas is not completed during the
    accounting period.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} If revenue is earned in consideration of the
    performance of exchange services, it shall be recorded in
    account 579 (Miscellaneous Operating Revenues).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [627]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Withdrawn from Underground Storage*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of gas withdrawn from underground
  storage and such cost shall be concurrently credited to account 152
  (Gas Stored Underground --- Available for Sale).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [628]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Delivered to Underground Storage* (*Credit*)
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall be credited with the cost of gas delivered to
  underground storage and such cost shall be concurrently debited to
  account 152 (Gas Stored Underground --- Available for Sale).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [629]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Used* (*Credit*)
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be credited with the cost of gas
    used for other than storage purposes and such cost shall be
    concurrently debited to operating expenses, construction or other
    accounts.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be subdivided as follows:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} gas used in construction;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} gas used in products extraction; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} gas used in operations or for other purposes.

##### [Underground Storage Operation]{.HTitleText3} {#underground-storage-operation .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [650]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Supervision*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the general supervision of the
    operation of underground storage facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Supervision to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 610(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [651]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Exploration and Development*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cost of labour, supplies and expenses
      incurred in the investigation, exploration and development of
      underground projects that do not prove feasible; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the net cost of drilling non-operative wells
      within an existing storage project.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the costs described in subsection (1) are
    material in relation to the net income of the year, the company may
    prepare and submit to the Commission, for its approval, an
    alternative method of accounting.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [653]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Wells*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the operation of underground storage system
      wells and facilities; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the drilling of wells used for storage
      purposes.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Wells to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    </div></li>

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Paintings, signs
    </div>
    </div></li>

    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Minor upkeep of well roads, fences
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Turning storage wells on and off
    </div>
    </div></li>

    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Moving cleaning-out tools between locations
    </div>
    </div></li>

    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Supplies such as gas, gasoline, oil used in pumping and bailing, lumber,
    nails, well swabs, chemicals, first aid supplies, hand tools and
    equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expenses such as use of automotive, trucking, aircraft and heavy work
    equipment, transportation of supplies, light, heat, water, telephone,
    telegraph, rents, employee travelling and subsistence, employee housing
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [654]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Gas Losses*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the amounts of inventory
    adjustments representing the cost of gas lost or unaccounted for in
    underground storage operations due to cumulative inaccuracies of gas
    measurement or other causes.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Adjustments relating to prior years shall be
    accounted for in accordance with section 78.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [655]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Field Line*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the operation of underground
    storage field line and facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Field Line to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 615(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [656]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Compressor*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the operation of underground
    storage system compressor stations and facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Compressor to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 616(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [657]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Measuring and Regulating*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the operation of underground
    storage system measuring and regulating facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Measuring and
    Regulating to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 617(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [658]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Purification*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the operation of underground
    storage system purification facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Purification to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 618(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [659]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Underground Storage Operations*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of labour, supplies and expenses
  incurred in the operation of underground storage system facilities not
  provided for elsewhere.

##### [Transmission Operation]{.HTitleText3} {#transmission-operation .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [660]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Supervision*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred at a division, district or similar
    field office in the general supervision of the operation of
    transmission system facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Supervision to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 610(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [663]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Transportation of Gas by Others*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of transportation charges paid to
  others where the charges are separate and not included as part of the
  delivery price of gas.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [664]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Communication*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the operation of facilities used
    wholly or predominantly in connection with the transmission
    communication system.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Communication to
    be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  ::: listItemLabel

  :::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,

    system pipeline and facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Pipeline to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    system compressor stations and facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Compressor to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  ::: listItemText2
  *Measuring and Regulating*

  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the operation of transmission
    system measuring and regulating stations and facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Measuring and
    Regulating to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

  Details are the same as for account 617(2).

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [669]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other Transmission Operations*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of labour, supplies and expenses
  that are incurred in the operation of the transmission system
  facilities and that are not provided for elsewhere.

  ::: listItemLabel
  [685]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *System Operation and Engineering*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred at the general offices of the company
    for the operation and engineering of the pipeline system.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of System Operation
    and Engineering to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these
    Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    <div class="listItemText0">

    and other employees of the general operating and engineering departments
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    <div class="listItemText0">

    </div>
