    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Office supplies and expenses such as stationery, printing, postage,
    maps, drawings, telephone, telegraph, light, heat, water
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Rents, janitor services and supplies, maintenance of office building and
    office equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [688]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other General Operations*
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall include the cost of labour, supplies and expenses
  that are incurred in the general operation of the pipeline system
  facilities and that are not provided for elsewhere.

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [689]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *General Operations Transferred* (*Credit*)
  :::
  :::::

  This account shall be credited with the costs of general operations
  transferred to other accounts, such as costs transferred to overhead
  charged to construction.

##### [Administrative and General Expense]{.HTitleText3} {#administrative-and-general-expense .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [721]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Administrative Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of salaries,
    supplies and expenses incurred in the general administration of the
    company where such costs are assignable to specific executive,
    administrative and general departments and are not chargeable to any
    other of the operating service functions listed in paragraphs
    12(4) (a) to (f) inclusive.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Administrative
    Expense to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Salaries and wages of officers, executives and other employees
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expenses of officers, executives and other employees such as travelling
    and subsistence, automotive, club and professional dues and expenses,
    and other incidental expenses
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Office supplies and expenses such as stationery, printing, postage,
    maps, drawings, telephone, telegraph, light, heat, water
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Rents, janitor services and supplies, maintenance of office building and
    office equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [722]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Special Services*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the fees and expenses of
    professional consultants and others for general services not
    applicable to any other particular operating functions. (*See*
    subsection 12(3) of these Regulations)

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Special Services
    to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Fees and expenses of auditors, accountants, appraisers, engineering
    consultants, management consultants, legal consultants, public relations
    and labour consultants
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Fees and expenses paid under contracts for general management services
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Pay and expenses of persons engaged for a special administrative or
    general purpose where the person so engaged is not considered an
    employee of the company
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [723]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Insurance*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the premium payable to
    insurance companies against any loss that may be sustained by the
    company.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the company provides self-insurance, the
    reserve accruals shall be debited to this account and concurrently
    credited to account 290 (Insurance Reserves). (*See* sections 21 and
    61 of these Regulations)

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Insurance to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Fire, storm, burglary, boiler explosion, lightning, riot and fidelity
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Public liability, casualty, property damage and employee liability
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [724]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Injuries and Damages*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include all expenses, including
    losses not recovered by insurance but excluding law expenses
    incurred in the settlement of injury and damage claims.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Injuries and
    Damages to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Losses not covered by insurance on account of injuries to employees or
    others and damages to property of others
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Fees and expenses of claims of outside investigators and adjusters
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Payments of awards to claimants for court costs and legal services
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Medical and hospital services and other expenses of claimants
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [725]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Employee Benefits*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} payments or contributions to provide pension in
      respect of past or current services of employees;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} salaries or wages paid to employees disabled by
      injuries after the employees have been off duty for a reasonable
      length of time;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} amounts paid or contributed for the relief or
      welfare of employees, including any expenses related thereto; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} premiums for group, life, medical, hospital,
      accident and sickness insurance.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the company has instituted a pension fund,
    trust fund or welfare fund, the contributions shall be debited to
    this account and transferred to such pension, trust or welfare fund.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the company does not institute a pension or
    trust fund but makes provision for a reserve against pension or
    welfare, the reserve accrual shall be debited to this account and
    concurrently credited to account 291 (Welfare and Pension Reserves).

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Employee Benefits
    to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Payments to or on behalf of employees or pensioners, and to pension
    funds or to insurance companies for pension purposes
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Payments for group and life insurance premiums
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Payments for medical, hospital and other accident and sickness expenses,
    either directly or to insurance companies or others
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Payments to employees disabled by accident or sickness when off duty for
    a reasonable length of time
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Savings plan contributions
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Expenses in connection with educational, recreational and social
    activities for the benefit of employees
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [728]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Other General Expense*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include expenses that are
    incurred in connection with the general management of the company
    and that are not provided for elsewhere.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Other General
    Expense to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Industry association dues for company memberships
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Contributions for conventions and meetings of the industry
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Contributions to the experimental and research work of the industry,
    including scholarships and similar grants
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Trustee, registrar, and transfer agents, fees and expenses and other
    fiscal or corporate expenses
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Stockholders’ meeting expenses
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Publishing and printing annual reports
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Advertising and promotion
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Donations
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Bad Debts
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [729]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Administrative and General Expense Transferred* (*Credit*)
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall be credited with the amounts of
    administrative and general expenses that are allocated to other
    accounts or to overhead during construction.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The method and basis of allocation referred to in
    subsection (1) shall be maintained in subsidiary records.

#### [Text Pertaining to Maintenance Expense Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#text-pertaining-to-maintenance-expense-accounts .SchedHeadL2}

##### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#general-5 .SchedHeadL3}

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} The total of the balances in the maintenance expense
  accounts shall be included in income account 302 (Maintenance
  Expenses).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the same account title is used in more than one
  group of maintenance expense accounts, the account text instructions
  are given in detail for the accounts under the gathering group.

##### [Gathering Maintenance]{.HTitleText3} {#gathering-maintenance .SchedHeadL3}

- ::::: listItemBlock1
  ::: listItemLabel
  [810]{.lawlabel} 
  :::

  ::: listItemText2
  *Supervision*
  :::
  :::::

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} This account shall include the cost of labour,
    supplies and expenses incurred at a division, district or similar
    field office in the general supervision of the maintenance of
    gathering system facilities.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} *Representative List of Items of Supervision to be
    included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)

    <figure>
    <ul>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">

    stenographers and other office employees
    </div>
    </div></li>
    <li><div class="listItemBlock1">
    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Travelling, subsistence, automotive and incidental expenses of employees
    </div>

    </div></li>

    <div class="listItemLabel">

    </div>
    <div class="listItemText0">
    Rents, janitor services and supplies, repairs of buildings and equipment
    </div>
    </div></li>
    </ul>
    </figure>

      activities such as power plant, extraction and refining units,
      laboratory and warehouse facilities.

    Refining to be included*. (*See* section 14 of these Regulations)
